<header>*( ) : ( 0 ) : ( )*</header>

That was great and wondrous. Finally, this figure may yet hold this mystery: when, after so many eternities and ages, all things shall be restored through the Son of God, and the great Jubilee Year <term>Jubilee Year: Derived from the biblical "Year of Jubilee" (Leviticus 25), this refers here to the "Great Year" or the final cosmic restoration when all debts are canceled and all things return to their original divine state</term>, the miracle of all miracles, shall be fulfilled—when all is once again united with God, and angels and humans alike shall have entered into rest in God, those who had divided themselves from God into so many <term>Schiedlichkeiten: A term for the various states of separation, diversity, or fragmentation that occurred when creatures turned away from the unified divine source</term> and found rest nowhere until they returned to their Center <note>original: *Centrum*</note>, and God is all in all. This can well be seen in this work as if in a mirror of the three worlds <note>Likely referring to the Threefold World: the dark world of fire/will, the light world of majesty, and the external world of the four elements</note>. May the great God, Creator and Sustainer of Heaven and Earth, grant us that we—within His light, as in the illuminated radiance of grace <note>original: *Gnaden-Schein*</note> of the Holy Spirit—may see Him as the true, perfect light, and that we may walk here as children of the light in the light, so that there we may be and remain eternally united with His light! May God the Father, Son, and Holy Spirit help us to achieve this; highly praised and glorified for all eternity! Amen.

->E N D.<-

## Preface.

Beloved and, according to the honors
due to your rank, esteemed reader. Among all
authors who have ever written about the
secret *Chemical Science* <note>original: "Chymischen Wissenschafft"; at this time, chemistry and alchemy were not yet distinct disciplines</note>,
none has—without wishing to boast—
expressed himself so distinctly, clearly, and brightly, neither in the
preparatory nor the follow-up work, as I have in
my two *little treatises*: the *Mineral
Glue* <term>Gluten: in alchemical texts, this refers to a cohesive, "sticky" substance or the essential "mercury" that binds metals</term>, and the *Philosophical Pearl-
Tree*, the *Growth of the 3 Principles* <note>These are the three "primes" of Paracelsian alchemy: Salt, Sulfur, and Mercury, which were believed to make up all material things</note>, and
then here in this *Treatise*, wherein
the secrets of the same are presented to the world
quite naked and revealed; which
all *Philosophers* <note>original: "Philosophi"; referring to the historical masters of alchemy who were often called "philosophers of nature"</note>
have covered up and only occasionally, as if
slumbering and sleeping, let a word slip,
and amused themselves, as it were, by telling
posterity such sweet dreams, through which
no one could guess the truth. Therefore, many
unfortunate workers have spent their money,
effort, and time in vain, and have fallen into
misfortune. Because I have now seen that
our life from you-

<summary>In this preface, the author asserts that his alchemical writings are uniquely clear and honest compared to other masters who used riddles and "dreams" to obscure their secrets. He cites his previous works on mineral substances and the three principles as evidence of his transparency.</summary>
<keywords>preface, alchemy, three principles, Mineral Glue, Pearl-Tree, philosophical secrets, clarity vs obscurity</keywords>

->❀  ):( 0 ):(  ❀<-

-th <note>completing the word "youth" from the previous page's fragment "you-"</note> onward until old age, a burdensome journey,
where we, so to speak, on a ship upon the
turbulent sea and contrary winds, are
pushed down toward the deepest and dark abyss of death;
for we have, from childhood onward, internal enemies,
namely whole heaps of burdensome diseases which
cannot be counted, and from the outside a terrifying
army of many inconveniences and accidents, whose
commander is **Poverty**.
Against these two adversaries now, who have
as it were conspired against our happiness and
made a secret pact against us, nothing has
hitherto been found to combat them but **Patience**;
However, the brave and untiring spirit of man
resists this misfortune, and does not allow itself
to be struck to the ground or overcome by so much
misfortune, but rather arms itself with the glorious
weapons of invincible **Wisdom**,
and searches through the heart of the world and of nature,
to see if he might find something that could slay these enemies
and do them harm. In these times of need, he prays
and calls out to **GOD** for enlightenment and for the spirit of wisdom,
so that he may recognize his way, and his works
may be pleasing to Him; then he follows confidently
the path of nature, because **GOD** has very large
**treasure chests** <note>original: "Schatz-Kasten"; a metaphor for the hidden resources and medicinal secrets found within the natural world</note> therein. When he now, united with

<summary>The author describes human life as a perilous sea voyage plagued by disease and poverty, arguing that while patience is a common defense, the human spirit uses wisdom and prayer to uncover God's hidden treasures in nature to defeat these ills.</summary>
<keywords>human condition, allegory of the sea, disease, poverty, patience, wisdom, secrets of nature, divine enlightenment</keywords>

->❁ ):( ( 0 ) ):( ❁<-

**GOD**, then **GOD** allows him to make use of His **treasure chests**, for He entrusts him with the **key to health and wealth**. These keys he should use for **GOD's** honor and for the benefit and service of his neighbor. Therefore, through **GOD's** grace, I have been permitted to hand these keys to my fellow Christian, so that he might be drawn toward **GOD** through love and good deeds, and be snatched from the **Sea of Anguish** of sickness and poverty, and might land at the safe port of the good land, and may no longer have cause to lament:

> I cannot pray because of the pains of my body; I cannot go to church because of the labor, hunger, and sorrow required to sustain myself and my own; I have no time to think of **GOD**; I must even go to hell because of the <term>worries of the stomach <note>original: "Bauch-Sorgen"; a phrase referring to the overwhelming anxiety of providing for basic physical survival</note></term>:

and more such impatient and unchristian speeches and thoughts. All of this, dear reader, moved me to write so openly and clearly, because the need of my neighbor went to my heart; therefore I have instructed him in each and every thing, named the <term>Matter <note>original: "Materia"; the raw substance used in the alchemical process</note></term>, and shown the <term>Solution <note>original: "Solution"; the process of dissolving or liquefying a solid substance</note></term> of the coarse body, and the <term>Separation</term> of the elements, which they <note>referring to other alchemical authors</note> have all kept secret; likewise the <term>Conjunction <note>original: "Conjunction"; the ritual or chemical marriage/reunion of separated elements</note></term> of the water and the earth, of the spirit with the body, of the body with soul, spirit, and body, the formation of the whi- <note>fragment of "weißen," likely referring to the "White Work" or Albedo stage of alchemy; the catchword "fen" suggests the full word was "weiffen" or "weissifen," to make white</note>

I could have certainly remained silent, for the knowledge would not have weighed upon my heart <note>meaning he felt no internal pressure to speak for his own sake</note>, just as I have sought neither honor nor praise through it; otherwise, I would have written my name in full if I had wished to be known to the world. Rather, this has been done out of compassionate and merciful love for my neighbor, because human life is short and full of toil, misery, and need, so that it might be sweetened by this <term>sugar <note>The author uses "sugar" as a metaphor for the alchemical medicine or "Elixir" that eases the bitterness of human suffering</note></term>.

Therefore, I ask the blessed worker of these things that he would use such knowledge for the honor of **God** and for the benefit of his poor neighbor. Let him not, by building churches, make for himself a—

In this way, he will find this treasure, which he bestows upon the poor, a thousandfold again in heaven, and hear this praise from **Christ** on that day: "Well done, you good and faithful servant; you have been faithful over a little, I will set you over much; enter into the joy of your Lord." <note>This is a citation of the Parable of the Talents from the Gospel of Matthew 25:21.</note>

To the diligent reader and worker, I wish luck, blessing, and prosperity from **God** in this endeavor! I remain committed to serving the God-loving reader further in a Christian spirit.

Given at Weimar,
->July 13, 1705.<-

## First Question.

### What is the Treasury of Nature?

**Answer.** It is the whole of nature itself: 1. According to the fiery dark world. 2. According to the watery elementary world. 3. According to the astral air-world, in which the stars and planets [reside]. 4. According to our world, which is called the mortal transitory world, as it stands now in its divided state, since **God** <term>God <note>original: "GOtt"</note></term> partitioned it into the four <term>elements <note>original: "Elementa"</note></term>, namely air, water, fire, and earth. Fire occupies the highest place within; after that, water; in the air is the light, the stars, and these <term>meteorological phenomena <note>original: "Meteora"; in this period, this referred to all atmospheric and celestial occurrences, including rain, wind, and lightning, not just "meteors" in the modern sense.</note></term> act upon the earth, which, like a <term>magnet</term>, draws the influences from the air to itself. 

However, to describe the whole of nature or all its forces and effects is not possible for any human; even if he lived a thousand years, he would still not explore nor experience it all. For in the fire-heaven or the fiery dark world above the stars, no man has been, although this <term>chaos <note>In alchemical and paracelsian philosophy, "Chaos" often refers to the primordial atmospheric space or the "great depth" that separates different realms of existence.</note></term> often opens itself, and fire has fallen from heaven, as seen during the overturning of **Sodom and Gomorrah**, and likewise when the **Prophet** <note>A reference to the prophet Elijah in 2 Kings 1:10–12, who called down fire from heaven.</note> let the fire of God fall from heaven, which had to consume the captains with their fifty men; fire has also fallen from heaven more frequently in our own times. 

We have also not been in the watery elementary world. Many consider such a place to be the so-called **Milky Way** in the heavens, in which so many millions of stars

<meta>catchword: stars</meta>

<summary>This page begins the treatise by defining the "Treasury of Nature" as a fourfold cosmological system, explaining how the earth acts as a magnet for celestial and elemental influences. It describes the unreachable nature of the fiery and watery realms, citing biblical examples of fire descending from the heavens.</summary>
<keywords>Treasury of Nature, four elements, cosmology, magnetism, celestial influence, Elijah, Sodom and Gomorrah, Milky Way, meteorological phenomena</keywords>

<term>fiery sphere <note>original: "feurigen Sphæræ"</note></term> for so long, so that it 
cannot mix with the light of the stars. In 
the <term>astral air-world <note>original: "astralischen Lufft-Welt"</note></term> no one has

such things with their eyes, their spirit has 
not ceased to investigate the course of the 
stars, their effects, and also to measure 
their size, which—concerning their effects 
and courses—is quite accurate; but to 
measure the size of such bodies, it seems 
to me, will not be accurate. But of what 
such bodies consist, no man has yet been 
able to discover, other than to say they 
are lights which shine. But that they are 
supposed to burn is not so; they give off 
no flame or smoke, although the sun is 
fire through and through, heats, warms, 
and also illuminates everything, and is the 
<term>spirit of the great world <note>original: "Geist der grossen Welt"; a reference to the Macrocosm or the universe as a living organism.</note></term>; 
yet this is no combustible fire, but rather 
the fire and light which previously illumi-

thinly; now, however, it is driven together 
into a narrow space and, as it were, concentrated, 
therefore it burns with <term>pain <note>original: "Schmertzen"; the author suggests the sun's heat is an "agonizing" or piercing intensity caused by the compression of light that was once diffused and gentle.</note></term>. 
But when, after the <term>renewal of the earth <note>original: "Verneurung der Erden"; a reference to the biblical "New Heaven and New Earth."</note></term>, 
the sun along with all the stars has 
returned into its <term>Ether <note>original: "Æther"</note></term>, then from this light 
the new heaven, in its division and 
thinning, will permeate the new heaven 
and new earth with the pure, ensouled light,

...water, fire, and earth mix together, and [there] shall be a heaven and a new earth, such that we will no longer remember the former. But as far as the nature of our earth is concerned, humans have a slightly better knowledge [of it], and the power of all nature consists of the four elements, which contains within itself three kingdoms: the animal, vegetable, and mineral. To describe these three kingdoms of nature according to their powers, effects, their many species, scent, taste, life, stirrings and movements, procreations, reproductions, and perishing, is likewise impossible, even though Solomon—who was the wisest of men—wrote of them all, even down to the hyssop <note>A small herb mentioned in the Bible (1 Kings 4:33) as the limit of Solomon's botanical knowledge.</note> that grows out of the wall. But where are his books? Were not most and best of his writings burned after his death by the command of a prophet, because of the idolatrous Jews? For he had described the whole <term>Magic <note>original: "Magiam"</note></term> and <term>Kabbalah <note>original: "Cabalam"; refers here to mystical Jewish traditions and esoteric knowledge of the divine and natural worlds.</note></term>, through which one could see into the hearts of all. Likewise, [he] recognized their power and nature, so that he even crafted a special root into a ring for the High Priest, which had been dug up according to its proper <term>influence <note>original: "Influenz"; in the context of Hermetic science, this refers to the "down-pouring" of celestial power from the stars into earthly objects at specific astrological moments.</note></term>; whenever the High Priest held the hand on which he wore the ring before the mouth of a possessed person, the Devil had to depart immediately. But because today few such highly enlightened people are to be found, little or nothing proper is written, but only poor and common sciences that only fill the belly; [they] only crawl around the edge and

--- Page 14 ---
<meta>This page continues from the previous page's discussion of King Solomon’s lost wisdom and the "influence" of celestial powers on earthly objects. The text transitions from the loss of ancient knowledge to a critique of current academic science compared to true, "inner" philosophy.</meta>

<header>❁ )( o )( ❁ 11</header>

...and the outermost part of things, is merely the science of the schools <note>original: "Schulen Wiſſenſchafft"; referring to conventional academic or scholastic learning of the time, which the author views as superficial.</note>; it wanders in constant uncertainty, darkness, and among doubtful matters, and gropes around with the staff of conjecture on the path of error, because almost no one knows the right way of nature, especially in medicine <note>original: "Medicin"</note>, where nature and its powers ought to be investigated. For this reason, modern medicines <note>original: "Medicamenta"</note> have such poor power against stubborn and fixed diseases compared to spagyric ones <note>original: "Spagiriſchen"; refers to alchemical medicines produced by the process of separating, purifying, and recombining elements to unlock their "inner" potency.</note>, which the schools do not teach, because their foundation does not lie in the center of things, but only in the external parts.

However, the true philosophy <note>original: "Philoſophia"</note>—which is also the gymnosophy <note>original: "Gymnoſophia"; a term for the "naked philosophers" of ancient India known for their asceticism and deep wisdom.</note> of the Indians, the magic <note>original: "Magia"</note> of the Egyptians, and the Kabbalah <note>original: "Cabala"</note> of the Jews—penetrates through to the innermost core and leaves nothing unexamined. It researches everything completely, bringing its power and effect to light, applying it to its proper use, and demonstrating the power of nature through nature itself. It strips and undresses veiled nature so that she may be recognized; for she is not so firmly hidden, and willingly allows herself to be found by those who pursue her on the right path of nature.

## II. What then is the secret treasury of nature?

**Answer.** It is nature laid bare and discovered in her anatomy <note>In this context, "anatomy" refers to the internal structure and spiritual essence of a substance, not just its physical parts.</note>, where one recognizes the innermost essence of things, and the workings and power of nature. And it is not enough for me to say: The fire or...

<summary>This page contrasts superficial academic "school science" with a deeper, universal philosophy that unites Indian, Egyptian, and Jewish traditions to reveal the "inner" powers of nature. It argues that true medicine must be based on this internal "anatomy" rather than external appearances.</summary>

### VII. What is the universal solvent? <note>original: "Menstruum universale"</note>

Answer: This is understood in many different ways: First, this is the most universal substance, the **acid** <term>Acidum: In this context, it refers to a vital, corrosive yet life-giving force in nature</term> of nature, the **aerial niter** <term>Nitrum aëreum: A hypothetical substance in the air thought to be necessary for life and combustion; a precursor to the scientific discovery of oxygen</term>, which we all draw into ourselves in an invisible way. Everything that has breath lives from it, and all **plants** <note>original: "Vegetabilia"</note> grow through it, because there is a hidden food and nourishment of life in the air. It is rightly called the Spirit and breath of God, for in him we live, and move, and have our being; as it is truly said: *He flies on the wings of the wind.* This **universal spirit** <note>original: "Spiritus universalis"</note> or **aerial niter** is always more abundant in one place than another, and also varies by time—such as in the spring, when the sun enters **Aries** <note>Widder</note>, and in the autumn in **Libra** <note>Wage</note>. Although it is present at all times, in the summer, when the sun stands in the highest house of heaven and moves in **Leo** [♌], this salt is not felt as much because it is too drawn up and dried out by the sun. In the harsh winter, it is also hardly felt and is only revived by the sun; but when the sun is in the **temperate zone** <note>original: "Zona temperata"</note>, as in spring and autumn, it is most keenly felt and perceived. The strongest magnets, however, which attract this spirit, are **Venus** [♀] <note>Copper</note> and **Mars** [♂] <note>Iron</note>—or rather their "kisses"—likewise, a **vitriolated magnesia** <term>Magnesia vitriolata: A term used historically for various sulfate minerals like Epsom salts</term>, **Hessian solar iron ore** <term>minera martis Solaris Hassiaca: A specific iron-bearing mineral source from the Hesse region of Germany prized by local alchemists</term>, alum, and **vitriol gravels** [⊕], every earth that carries "iron-seeds" within it is impregnated by this **hungry salt** <term>Sale esurino: An alchemical term for a corrosive, acidic salt that "hungers" to dissolve or "eat" metals</term>. When water subsequently flows through such channels, there arise...

X. Thus, **Aqua Regia** <term>AR.: Aqua Regia; a highly corrosive mixture of acids known for its ability to dissolve gold</term>, **Aqua Fortis** <term>AF.: Aqua Fortis; nitric acid</term>, **Spirit of Salt** <term>Spiritus Salis: Hydrochloric acid</term>, and the like, are not the right **solvents** <term>Menstrua: Alchemical liquids used to dissolve or extract the essence of a solid substance</term> for **metals**, if one wishes to make medicines or **tinctures** <note>concentrated medicinal or alchemical extracts</note> from them.

Answer: No; rather, they are merely common **dissolving-waters** <note>original: "Solvir-Wasser"</note> that are not of a metallic nature; they can also be washed away from the metals again and separated from them by evaporation. Nor are **Salt of Tartar** <note>Potassium carbonate</note> or **Spirit of Wine** <note>Ethanol/Alcohol</note> suitable, for metals do not grow from any of these. One finds no Spirit of Wine or Salt of Tartar within metals; instead, a **hungry salt** <term>Sal esurinum: A sharp, acidic salt found in mines, believed by alchemists to be the natural "appetite" or solvent that allowed minerals to grow and change</term>, known as the **Acid of Nature**, is found there in the mines. It aids in sticking and soaking; it dissolves and coagulates itself within the metals.

XI. Is this acidic spirit then the **universal solvent** <note>original: "Menstruum universale"</note>?

Answer: It is the universal **Acid**, through which everything grows, stirs, lives, and moves, drawing such a spirit into itself in an invisible manner. However, that this **Salt** should be the universal solvent for the metallic kingdom, through which they are "reborn" into **tinctures**, that is not the case. If one cannot find the right metallic **solvent** <term>Menstruum: A liquid used in alchemy to dissolve or extract the essence of a solid substance</term>, one must use this [Acid of Nature] to make medicines from the metals, and also from the minerals, because this **Spirit** <note>In this context, a "spirit" refers to a volatile, refined essence or vapor</note> is already medicine enough on its own. 

When it is united with metals, however—wherein its **Acid** <note>original: "Acidum"</note> is broken and "killed" <note>To "kill" an acid in alchemy means to neutralize its corrosive sharpness so it no longer "bites"</note> so that it becomes sweet and drinkable—it possesses extraordinary powers. When it is then distilled and becomes spiritualized, it is even more magnificent. 

But if one were to let metals be violently devoured in **Aqua Fortis** <term>AF.: Nitric acid</term>, **Aqua Regia** <term>AR.: A mixture of acids capable of dissolving gold</term>, or **Spirit of Salt** <term>Spiritus Salis: Hydrochloric acid</term>, who would want to drink this poison and death? Or should a sweetness come from it, like milk and honey? Salt remains salt, no matter how often it is distilled as a spirit; when it stands for a long time and grows old, it becomes physical salt again. This is also true of **gold** <note>original symbol: ☉</note>: if one lets it stand for a long time, the solvent drops the **gold** or metal and remains alone. The same happens with **silver** <note>original symbol: ☾</note>, which crystallizes again as **silver** and never truly unites with the metals, but only clings to them from the outside, eating away the pure and the impure parts together.

XII. But are there not many rare **medicines** <note>original: "Medicamenta"</note> to be made from metals and minerals which are **dissolved** <note>original: "solvirt"</note> by **Aqua Fortis** <term>AF.: Nitric acid, a strong corrosive</term>, **Aqua Regia** <term>AR.: A mixture of nitric and hydrochloric acids, famous for its ability to dissolve gold</term>, **Spirit of Wine** <note>original: "Spiritus vini"; concentrated alcohol or ethanol</note>, and similar corrosives?

**Answer:** Yes, they can be made from them if the **corrosive** <note>The harsh acidic solvent</note> is cleanly separated from the substance and corrected by **Spirit of Wine**; however, all these **medicines** only reach the "first digestion" <note>original: "Däuung"; in alchemy, "digestion" refers to the process of heating a substance gently to mature or break it down. The "first digestion" implies a superficial or incomplete level of refinement</note>, and therefore their effect is not great. Whoever does not know better **solutions** <note>original: "Solutiones"</note> must be satisfied with these, and act like the **suitors of Penelope** <note>In Homer’s Odyssey, the suitors wait for the queen but distract themselves by sleeping with her maids; here, it is a metaphor for an alchemist who settles for lesser results instead of reaching the "true bride" or the Great Work</note>, dallying with the maidservants until he obtains the true bride.

XIII. Is the true **universal solvent** <term>Menstruum universale: A legendary "alkahest" or solvent capable of dissolving all bodies into their primary liquid matter</term> for the metallic kingdom also difficult to make?

**Answer:** To the ignorant it is difficult, but to the knowledgeable it is clear and easy; yet it requires much labor, and the clever worker must be like the strong **Hercules** who overcomes everything.

XIV. **Basilius Valentinus** <note>A legendary 15th-century Benedictine monk and alchemist</note> has described such a work in **12 Keys**, and **Ripley** <note>George Ripley (c. 1415–1490), a famous English alchemist</note> in **12 Gates**; are there then 12 keys and 12 gates, what are they named, and what do they unlock?

**Answer:** There are 12 keys, 12 doors, and 7 seals. But whoever unlocks the first door with the right key can thereafter unlock all the other doors with that same key, one after another, and not...

projected upon the metals, whereby one part

Beyond this, they have described nothing further.

so that it becomes thick and hard through it.

can ponder other and greater secrets: how he shall, from this work or **Wheel of Nature**, begin a new creation and generation, since the preceding [work] is minor and as nothing compared to this heavenly and angelic creature. And when such movement and revolution of the heavens comes again to an end and stands still, the artist brings it once more to rest; he can thus for all eternity raise and give new birth to this new heaven and this new earth <note>In alchemy, "heaven" and "earth" often symbolize the volatile (rising) and fixed (settling) parts of the substance</note>, and always in a shorter time. Where it previously required several months, thereafter it takes only one, then several weeks, days, and hours, and finally several times within a single hour, so that this wheel—this heaven and earth—becomes flowing <note>original: "lauffend"; likely referring to the substance becoming liquid or "running" during the process</note>, passing through all the colors, and then stands still again and ceases its driving. Thus it is elevated through all eternity by the single **active principle** <note>original: "agens"</note> and **passive principle** <note>original: "patiens"</note>, and its power increases infinitely. No artist, for all eternity, can fathom the end of Nature’s potential, just as little as they can fathom **God** and eternity itself. This is the **Multiplication into Infinity** <note>original: "Multiplication in Infinitum"</note>. Whether they [the authors] knew this and did not describe it, I do not know. It may be that they were satisfied with the **tincture** <term>A potent alchemical liquid used for transmutation</term> which transformed several thousand parts, and did not reflect further on the craft, or perhaps they knew it and kept silent, leaving it to the reader for further contemplation.

<meta>This page continues the discussion of "Multiplication" from page 23. While the previous page described the infinite potential of the alchemical work, this page addresses a common failure: how an artist might successfully create the Stone but ruin it during the attempt to increase its power.</meta>

<page-num>24</page-num>

->❁  ):( (o) ):(  ❁<-

XVII. Many say, however, that some have made a **Tincture** <term>A concentrated alchemical substance capable of transforming base metals into gold or silver</term> or the **Stone** <note>The Philosopher's Stone</note>, but they were not capable of the **Multiplication** <note>The process of increasing the quantity or the transformative power of the Stone</note>. Consequently, they ruined the entire Tincture; whereas it had previously transformed one thousand parts, it afterwards did nothing or very little. And yet, is it not said that the Tincture or the Stone cannot be corrupted?

Answer: I truly believe it, for if they contaminate the pure, refined Tincture with impure leaden and arsenical spirits, it is no different than if one crushed a precious gemstone and mixed it into coarse brick-dust and then threw it all together. Who would now extract this gemstone again from this earth and filth? Or when they unite it with the **vivifying spirit** <note>The life-giving principle or "soul" of the matter</note>, they mix it incorrectly; where they should take the red, they take the white <note>In alchemical color theory, white corresponds to the "Small Work" (silver) and red to the "Great Work" (gold); using the wrong one halts progress</note>. Instead of the **dry water** <term>A paradoxical term for "philosophical mercury"—a substance that is liquid like water but does not wet the hands</term>, which is also called ointment or oil, they take raw and unripe wet **menstruums** <note>original: "Menstrua"; chemical solvents used to dissolve solids</note>. Through these, they extinguish the fire of the Tincture. They add the unripe, raw, and unboiled to that which is mature and boiled. Since such a fiery body requires fire for its nourishment, they instead give it **Virgin’s Milk** <note>An alchemical term for a specific white liquid or solvent used during the early "infancy" of the chemical process</note>, which was previously only used for the raising of this "child," but which is no longer needed now that it has become a strong man.

<summary>This page explains why alchemists often fail during the Multiplication phase by using the wrong materials or solvents, effectively "starving" or "drowning" the mature Tincture with substances meant only for its earlier stages.</summary>
<keywords>Multiplication, Tincture, Philosopher's Stone, leaden spirits, arsenical spirits, dry water, menstruums, Virgin's Milk, alchemical failure</keywords>

XVIII. If one must unlock so many doors before entering the **Treasury of Nature** <term>A metaphorical storehouse of alchemical secrets and the hidden potential within physical matter</term>, what does one see in the first room?

Answer: There is nothing beautiful to see therein; it is an **old leper** <note>In alchemical symbolism, the 'old man' or 'leper' represents the raw, unrefined base metal—often lead or gold in its 'dead' or impure state—which must be 'cleansed' of its impurities (the leprosy)</note> in a bath, full of scales, spots, and leprosy, who is being purified by the bath so that he is cleaned of his scales and spots, and his body emerges pure and healthy.

XIX. Is this the old man’s internal purification, and with what kind of water is he bathed, and how long is he in the bath?

Answer: This bath only washes the body clean externally; internally this water does nothing, as it lacks the power to **separate the pure from the impure** <note>original: "purum ab impuro"; a core axiom of alchemy focusing on the isolation of the essential substance</note>. It is the bath of nature and her **vinegar** <note>Likely 'philosophical vinegar,' a sharp, acidic solvent used to break down materials</note>. As for the time, many **unnecessary steps** <note>original: "superflua"</note> are taken here; one can certainly accomplish it in a few days, yet many a person plagues himself for half a year with this **solution** <term>The alchemical stage of 'solutio,' where a solid is dissolved into a liquid state</term>.

XX. What is done further with this old man?

Answer: One presses out his water-blisters and sores so that his body becomes dry and **blood-red**. The stinking water which evaporated from him and smoked out of all his limbs—from hands, feet, head, and hair—is taken after its **rectification** <term>The process of refining a liquid, usually through repeated distillation, to increase its purity and potency</term>, and he is bathed once again...

this body within, then his blood <note>original: "Geblüth"</note> will be purified, beautiful, red, and of a **sanguine temperament** <term>In the theory of the four humors, the sanguine temperament—associated with blood—was considered warm, moist, and lively, contrasting with the cold, dry 'melancholic' nature of Saturn</term>, where previously it was dry, hard, and parched, after the fashion of the **Old lame limping Saturn** [♄].

**XXI. Is this then the second key?**
**Answer:** It is indeed, and it comes from the first key, as it is that with which one must unlock the second door; thus it is found that this **Old Man** carries a small, beautiful, and pure child in his body. He himself, however, is **Vulcan** <note>The Roman god of fire; here representing the transformative heat of the furnace</note>, the snake or dragon, who releases nothing but fire and smoke from his mouth, nose, ears, hands, feet, and all limbs.

**XXII. Is this then Jupiter [♃]?**
**Answer:** No, it is the stone which **Saturn** [♄] swallowed instead of his son **Jupiter** [♃], and must spit out again <note>This refers to the Greek myth where Saturn (Cronus) ate his children, but was tricked into eating a stone; alchemically, this represents the material absorbing and then releasing the 'Stone' through distillation</note>; that is, to transfer the stone into a volatile state. If Saturn does not swallow such a stone and spit it out again, he is not fit for the **Work** <term>The 'Great Work' or *Magnum Opus*, the process of creating the Philosopher's Stone</term>.

**XXIII. Now that Saturn [♄] has swallowed the Stone [⊕] and**
->**spit it out again, what should**<-
->**be done thereafter?**<-

**Answer:** One should purify the **four children** or **four elements**. The fire is the **element of fire** <note>original: "elementum ignis"</note>; from the **red Venus** [♀]—not from the common one which is green—is Pluto <note>Venus usually represents copper; 'red Venus' likely refers to a specific purified state or oxide, while 'green' refers to common verdigris</note>. Neptune is the God of the Sea or Water; Jupiter [♃] is the God in the Air, the Highest; and the Earth is Saturn [♄].

XXIV. Is then the previous *Saturn* not the right *Saturn*, and is there yet another?

**Answer.** The previous Saturn [♄] carries the true one within his body like a beautiful little child; and only this is the philosophical *Saturn*, which is revealed after the separation of the four elements, from the <term>quintessence</term> <note>Literally the 'fifth essence'; the purest and most concentrated substance extracted from the four elements</note> of the four elements. The previous Saturn [♄] was of the coarse earth, created by nature and available to every craftsman; the philosophical *Saturn* must be brought forth by the Sons of Art <note>A traditional term for alchemists who use 'Art'—skill and laboratory practice—to perfect what nature left incomplete</note>. This one has its beginning from the *Spirit* of Mercury [☿], which rises out of the sea from *Neptune*. I have also said long ago that all metals grow out of common elemental water and are the fruits of <term>meteors</term> <note>In historical alchemy, 'meteors' referred to any atmospheric or vaporous phenomena, including the rising and falling of gases within a sealed vessel</note>; the philosophical metals likewise grow out of our elemental water. Just as many metals as grow through nature, so many do we also have through Art; and Art can reach higher than nature, which can go no further than the generation of metals and precious stones, for nature possesses only the coarse *elements*. We, however, have <term>impregnated</term> <note>Saturated or fertilized the base material with a higher essence</note> the coarse elemental water with one of the most perfect bodies, which comes from the *prime entity* <note>original: "primo ente"; refers to the 'first matter' or fundamental substance of the universe</note> of the Sun [☉] and Moon [☽], and also of all planets. This we have purified a first, second, and third time, and separated into the four elements. Now there remains the separation of the *pure from the impure* <note>original: "purum ab impuro"</note>, which transcends the elements; from these elements we reveal the metals—these seven metals which grow from them and are far more magnificent bodies than the common ones. From such as these we also make precious
->stones,<-

stones, when we dissolve them again in their water, are very different from common ones; for ours are <term>Tinctures</term> <note>Highly concentrated substances believed to have the power to "tinge" or permanently change the color and nature of other materials</note>, which transform common metals into Gold [☉] <note>The symbol ☉ represents the Sun and, by extension, the most perfect metal, Gold</note>, and transform crystals or other lesser stones into bright, shining precious gems. # XXV. *But this description is a very tedious labor, whereas one reads in other books that the stone is made*
->*in 2 nights and 3 days?*<-

**Answer.** The reason that the stone seems so tediously described here is because no person in the world has described the <term>Preliminary Work</term> <note>original: "Vor-Arbeit"; the initial, often messy stages of cleaning and preparing raw materials which many authors skipped</note>; instead, they have all begun by writing of the <term>Separation of the Elements</term>, giving the seeker no light beforehand on how the material should be purified and made ready so that it *can* be separated into its <term>Elements</term>, except perhaps when a single word escaped them. Because I have seen that the matter is difficult enough as it is, and that not one person among a thousand is granted the ability to untie this <term>Gordian Knot</term> <note>original: "Nodum Gordium"; a legendary ancient puzzle used here to describe a problem so complex it seems impossible to solve</note>, I have not wished to withhold this from my fellow man out of compassion and mercy. That, however, the stone should be made...

...are accomplished in 2 nights and 3 days, should be understood in this way: it becomes black and dark twice; the 3 days occur when it becomes light, white, and shining. The second day is when the Sun rises with beautiful yellow rays. The third day is when it appears red and bloody. <note>This is a symbolic description of the "Four Stages" of alchemy: Nigredo (blackness), Albedo (whiteness), Citrinitas (yellowness), and Rubedo (redness). The author is explaining that "days" and "nights" refer to color changes in the flask, not literal 24-hour periods.</note>

-> **XXVI. Are the previous ones then the right** <-
-> **keys?** <-

**Answer.** They are the right keys, as they have unlocked only the outer nature, and then the gates and the elements; however, in the future, the secret keys to the cabinet and the secret treasury of nature shall follow.

-> **XXVII. What then are the names of the secret keys** <-
-> **and how many of them are there?** <-

**Answer.** There are 3 of them. The first is called <term>Dissolution</term> <note>original: "Solutio"; the process of turning a solid into a liquid or "opening" a metal</note>. The second is <term>Conjunction</term> <note>original: "Conjunctio"; the union of the "male" and "female" principles or opposites</note>. The third is called <term>Fixation</term> <note>original: "Fixatio"; making a substance stable so that it no longer evaporates or changes when heated</note>. The first one unlocks the inner chamber of the earth and is further divided into many keys, such as <term>Dissolution</term> [Solutio], <term>Coagulation</term> <note>original: "Coagulatio"; thickening a liquid into a solid</note>, <term>Sublimation</term> <note>original: "Sublimatio"; heating a substance until it rises as vapor and solidifies again at the top of the vessel</note>, <term>Distillation</term> [Distillatio], <term>Conjunction</term> [Conjunctio], and when the soul is drawn out from the <term>body</term> <note>original: "Cörper"</note> by the spirit and separated from the soul and spirit in the dissolving; therefore it is also called <term>Separation</term> [Separatio] and <term>Putrefaction</term> <note>original: "Putrefactio"; a stage of rot or decay where the material turns black, seen as a necessary "death" before purification</note>. The second key, <term>Conjunction</term>, brings the tincturing <note>staining or permeating</note> and coloring spirit into the new <term>body</term> <note>original: "Corpus"</note>. The third, <term>Fixation</term>, shapes everything toward eternal permanence.

-> **explained even better?** <-

**Answer.** In the second work, we will deal with

...on the **composition of elements**, how to <term>fix</term> <note>original: "figiren"; to make a volatile substance stable so it no longer evaporates under heat</note> them and bring them to a stable state. The greatest secret is <term>Augmentation</term> <note>original: "Augmentation"; the process of increasing the quantity or the transformative power of the alchemical medicine</note>. First, we compose the elements and let them putrefy so that they may receive a new life. We dissolve what has putrefied; we divide what has been dissolved; we reunite what has been purified, and fix them together into a solid state. In our raw **Mercury** [☿], our red dry **Gold** [☉] is dissolved, at which point it takes on the colors of a **rainbow** <note>This refers to the *cauda pavonis* or "peacock's tail" stage, where many colors appear in the flask</note> when the **Sulfur** [🜍] enters the water. This rainbow remains in the water, unlike in the other work where it appears in the air. After 40 days, this is distilled, during which the <term>terra damnata</term> <note>Latin: "condemned earth"; the useless, burnt-out residue or dross that remains at the bottom of the vessel after distillation</note> falls to the bottom.

In the other work, the soul must become a spirit, and the spirit a soul, and these two together must become one body. This body must pass through three **circulations**, and each circulation is a "Sun." The first **Sun** [☉] has a black face, the second a white one, and the third a red one. <note>These represent the three main stages of alchemy: the Black Work (Nigredo), the White Work (Albedo), and the Red Work (Rubedo).</note>

The first part of the **Stone** is the earthly **Gold** [☉]. Without earthly gold, the Stone cannot be made; first, it is divided into two parts. The other part of the Stone is **Mercury** [☿]. When it is still unprepared and fluid, it can dissolve bodies because it penetrates deep within them and clings fast. It is endowed with great power; it makes its own gold thin and brings it back into itself, into its <term>first matter</term> <note>original: "erste Materiam"; the primordial substance from which all things were thought to be created</note>. No other thing in the world possesses this power. Therefore, it is rightly said that in Mercury is found what the wise men seek, and within it are the riches of the entire work. It dissolves its own gold, it makes it soft, and it draws the soul out of the body,

The **Knowledge of the Stone** is so high and glorious that within it all of Nature, all things in the entire world, can be seen as in a clear mirror. For it possesses the nature of both the small world <note>the microcosm, or man</note> and the great world <note>the macrocosm, or the universe</note>, containing the four **Elements** and the **Quintessence** <note>the "fifth essence" or purest spiritual core of matter</note>, which one calls Heaven. In this Heaven, another Sun has established its seat. Some **Philosophers** <note>original: *Philosophi*; here meaning alchemists</note> compare this to an image of the clear Deity, which is neither of Heaven nor of Earth; they have called it the **Soul of the World** <note>original: *Seele der Welt* or *Anima Mundi*; the vital force that connects all living things</note>, or the intermediate nature. And just as GOD is present everywhere in the whole world, so too is this **Essence** everywhere in the philosophical world, within the vial or glass. Thus, this thing—which seems so insignificant—reaches to the very ends of the great world by producing its own kind. After His Son, God created nothing more glorious than this thing, and no **uninitiated person** <note>original: *Idiot*; used here in the classical sense of one who lacks specialized knowledge</note> should blaspheme it if they do not understand it.

The first is a **Reduction** <note>the process of returning a substance to its primary, simple state</note> of the imperfect body into a **Liquid** or **Chaos** <note>original: *Liquorem* or *Chaos*; the "prime matter" or disorganized state before structure is applied</note>; the second **Solution**...

<meta>This page continues the technical breakdown of the "Great Work," specifically detailing the various types of "Solution" or dissolution. It follows the previous page's introduction to the operations required to create the Philosopher's Stone.</meta>

<page-num>32</page-num>
<header>✻ ):( ( 0 ) ):( ✻</header>

---

...of the elements is a separation or setting apart from its **chaos** <note>In alchemy, "chaos" refers to the prime, unrefined matter before it is organized into elements</note> through distillation into the elements, namely into <term>Salt</term> <note>original symbol: 🜔</note>, <term>Sulfur</term> <note>original symbol: 🜍</note>, and <term>Mercury</term> <note>original symbol: ☿; collectively known as the Tria Prima</note>, or into Body, Soul, and Spirit, or into Water, Fire, Air, and Earth—the Air being the Oil. Thirdly, the **philosophical dissolution** is a dissolving of the Male and a thickening of the Female, occurring simultaneously during the stage of **putrefaction** <note>original: *Fäulung*; the stage where the matter "dies" and turns black before its purification</note>. Fourthly, the **dissolution of the ferment** is a reduction or restoration of the sulfurs of the luminaries <note>original: *Luminarium Schweffeln*; the "luminaries" are the Sun (Gold) and the Moon (Silver)</note>, into either white or red oil. Fifthly and finally, there is a **dissolution of the Stone** and of both types of elixirs; this increases their power and effectiveness. Finally, in the entire Work there are only two main categories or types: **Dissolution** and **Calcination** <note>original: *Solution* and *Calcination*; the liquid and dry paths of chemistry</note>. All other operations are sub-species of these categories. The first, Dissolution and **Moistening** (or rising up), is a work of the heavens; the latter, Calcination and **Drying**, is a work of the earth. Furthermore, both the white and the red elixirs are of two kinds: one is called the **Elixir of Strangers** <note>original: *Elixir Peregrinorum*; likely referring to a version of the medicine meant for common use or external substances</note>, and the other is the Elixir that has been exalted, elevated, or transformed into an oil.

XXX. But when we speak of <term>Mercury</term> <note>original symbol: ☿</note>, what is it actually?

**Answer:** Firstly, when Mercury is spoken of, it is not common quicksilver, but our **raw spirit**. In the second sense, it is our **calcining water**, which dissolves all bodies. Thirdly, it is the Mercury of the Body which it has dissolved—the body of the white <term>Sulfur</term> <note>original symbol: 🜍</note>. The fourth is the Mercury or the natural Sulfur.

<meta>catchword: or</meta>

<summary>This page defines five specific types of alchemical "solution" and categorizes the entire chemical art into the dual processes of dissolution (moistening) and calcination (drying). It further clarifies that "Mercury" in these texts refers to internal spirits and powerful solvents rather than the common metal quicksilver.</summary>

<keywords>Dissolution, Calcination, Tria Prima, Sulfur, Mercury, Salt, Putrefaction, Elixir of Strangers, Luminaries, Philosophical Solution</keywords>

...or **Sulfur** <note>original symbol: 🜍</note> nature. Firstly, the **Mercury** <note>original: Merc.; referring to the alchemical spirit or "water"</note> is a constantly remaining water extracted from the bodies through distillation or dissolution, through sublimation, and through subtilization. 2. There are four Mercuries: a raw one, a sublimated one, a **Mercury of Magnesia** <note>original: ☿ magnesia; here "magnesia" refers to a prime mineral material, not the modern chemical</note>, which belongs to the Sulfur, and a "smeary" or oily one, which is a tingeing Mercury. Regarding its **solidification** <note>original: Congelatio</note>: one must solidify the Mercury with the "Body of Magnesia." This is not the common quicksilver, nor the magnesia that one sees with the eyes; rather, by "Mercury" we understand the fruits of this mixture, which is the **radical moisture** <note>original: humidum radicale; the fundamental life-fluid of matter</note>. This quicksilver is a spirit, and is called the "remaining water," or the "vinegar," the **solvent** <note>original: Menstruum</note>, or the **nourishment** <note>original: Nutrimentum</note> of the Sulfur. It comes out of the body and is of a white color. This **Sulfur** <note>original symbol: 🜍</note> must be composed or prepared, and what comes from it is the **Mercury of the Philosophers** <note>original: ☿ philosophorum</note>, which has been turned into a white, dry body. From this comes afterwards the "Divine" or "Heavenly" substance. And thus it is also with the **Red Oil of the Philosophers** <note>original: rothen Oleo Philosophorum</note>, or its liquid, and regarding the "incombustible oil," this is understood as the oil of the **ferments** <note>original: Fermenten, or Höfel ("yeast")</note>, before they are Sulfur, or when they are Sulfur; [extracted] from their oils or dissolution, from the red Mercury or "fire of the stone." Soul and Oil, red **Tincture**—they are one and the same. The white Mercury is the "Air and Water of the Stone." The Oil—its Fire and Soul—remains behind at the bottom of the vessel. The Earth is called "the Earth" when it is black; after its **calcination** <note>the process of reducing a substance to ash through high heat</note> it is snow-white. Then the salt is extracted...

...drawn off, after the second **separation of elements** <note>the process of breaking a substance down into its primary components</note>, the earth will remain behind white; then it is the salt. Also, sometimes the salt is taken from the white **Salt** <note>original symbol: 🜔; likely referring to the purified mineral base</note>, and the **Salt** <note>original symbol: 🜔</note> is used for the salt, and sometimes for the **Body** <note>original: Corpus</note> of all fixed salts. It is the **Body** and the house or dwelling, and is extracted from the **Body** of the metals. First it becomes ash, then a salt; from this salt comes the **Mercury of the Philosophers** <note>original symbol: ☿; the purified spirit of the work</note>, the white **Salt** <note>original symbol: 🜔</note>, and afterwards the **White Stone** <note>the penultimate stage of the Philosopher's Stone, capable of transmuting metals into silver</note>. The Art always requires its own earth, in which the greatest power and virtue lie to harden the **Mercury** <note>original symbol: ☿</note>, because this [mercury] always flows; afterwards, the spirit and the soul are returned to the body.

->XXXI. I would also like to hear more extensively about the **Conjunction**?<-
<note>Conjunction is the "chemical wedding," the stage where the separated male/female or fixed/volatile principles are reunited.</note>

Answer: Indeed, I have already spoken much about it; it is threefold. The first is of man and woman, from its **Body** <note>original: Corpore</note>, which previously was called a **Terra** <note>Latin: Earth</note> or Earth, by which the salt is understood. It is also called the **Matrimonium** <note>Latin: Marriage</note>, the wedding. The **Putrefaction** <note>the stage of decay and blackening</note> coagulates the earth with the heavy water; it hardens without any help or addition. One should marry the "dear son" to his "white sister." The son is the white earth, the sister is the white **Mercurial Water**. When they are boiled together for a time, they become one thing, which is called "the Good." When it **sublimes** <note>rises as vapor and solidifies</note>, it is the "foliated earth" <note>also called "Earth of Leaves," a flaky white crystalline substance</note>; but before this occurs, the **Putrefaction** <note>original: Fäulung; decay</note> must happen. From this ash the **Phoenix** <note>a symbol of rebirth and the perfected Stone</note> is born, which afterwards through the fire...

<meta>This page continues the discussion of the "Conjunction" or alchemical union. The previous page ended by defining the first conjunction as the union of male and female principles originating from the "Earth" (Terra). This page details the four specific stages of composition and conjunction required to reach the "Perfection" of the Philosopher's Stone, correlating these stages with the zodiac signs of Gemini and Leo.</meta>

<page-num>35</page-num>
<header>):( 0 ):(</header>
<sig>Ccc 2</sig>

...burnt: This first **composition** <note>the initial combining of materials</note> occurs from the moisture and dryness of the water and the earth, and is performed before the dryness enters into **putrefaction** <note>the stage of decomposition or "blackness" in the Great Work</note>. The earth is the **ferment** <note>original: Fermentum; a substance that causes a chemical change or "leavens" the matter</note> of the water, and the water is the **yeast** <note>original: Höfel; a catalyst for rising or growth</note> of the earth. This is called the first composition, without which the **Stone** <note>the Philosopher's Stone</note> cannot be made, and without which that which has been dissolved is at once brought to the **Elixir**. 

The second **conjunction** <note>the "chemical wedding" or union of opposites</note> is of the double body of the **foliated earth** <note>original: geblätterten Erden; a purified, flaky state of the matter</note> with the spirit, and the unification into **body, soul, and spirit**, once the white **Sulfur** <note>original symbol: 🜍</note> has grown. 

The third is the unification with the new red **Mercury** <note>original symbol: ☿</note>, which is called a feeding, a crushing, or **inceration** <note>original: Inceratio; the process of softening a substance to a wax-like consistency by adding liquid</note>. 

The fourth is the unification of the **Sulfur** <note>original symbol: 🜍</note> or **Stone** with its **ferment**, or **yeast-oil**, so that the **Stone** may be fermented. Those who are ignorant, who understood the "two mercuries" to mean the red **Mercury** <note>original symbol: ☿</note>, have thus deceived themselves; for the **philosophical mercuries** <note>original symbol: ☿</note> are the waters of the **Sun** and the **Moon** <note>alchemical gold and silver in liquid/spiritual form</note>. 

Nevertheless, these **conjunctions** occur: of the body with the earth, of the earth with spirit and body, and then the assembly of the souls with soul, spirit, and body; this is the **triple conjunction**. 

The first degree of **putrefaction** is followed by **desiccation** <note>drying out</note> by the air; when the **Stone** is dried and **incerated** <note>softened with oil/liquid</note>, then the third degree begins after it, and lasts until the **perfection** of the **Stone**, when the **Sun** runs in **Leo** <note>original symbol: ♌</note>. This is the highest house of heaven, the **Sun's** own house, of its dignity and glory; the previous stages were in **Gemini** <note>original: Zwilling</note>, which also made the colors black.

composite corrosive **Sulfur**-waters <note>original symbol: 🜍; refers to acidic liquids used to dissolve metals</note>; however, the **Sulfurs** <note>original symbol: 🜍</note> of the imperfect as well as the perfect are both white and red; the imperfect are still in the form of a liquid. ### XXXIII. What is then the Philosophers' Stone?

Answer: It is the <term>quintessence</term> <note>the "fifth essence" or purest spiritual core of matter, beyond the four physical elements</note> from the elements, a generated and renovated body that rejuvenates itself like the kingfisher <note>original: Eiß-Vogel; in alchemical lore, the kingfisher was sometimes associated with the calm required for the "Great Work" or the transition from volatile to fixed</note>, burns like the phoenix, but from the ashes becomes a new living body. It is the greatest secret of all nature, through which we can look into the heart of all creatures and of all nature; the stone of wonders, the mirror in which all nature is discovered, the stone of health and wealth, the union of heaven with the earth, the powers of the above and the below, the wonder of nature and art.

### XXXIV. But are the previous three secret keys used further, and what doors do they unlock?

Answer: One unlocks the center of the pure

...[opens] the **Virgin Earth** <note>original: Jungfräulichen Erden; refers to the primordial, pure matter from which the Stone is made</note>, which is otherwise so firmly and diamond-hard guarded that it cannot be conquered with any mallet, and all arrows from all corrosive spirits are shot at it in vain; this earth can be opened by nothing but this key. Afterward, the whole earth is transparent, the first **magical mirror**, so that one can recognize all fruits of the earth and see into everyone's heart. The third key, known as <term>Fixation</term> <note>the process of making a volatile substance stable under heat so it no longer evaporates</note>, makes it permanent and everlasting, so that one can enter this garden as often as one wishes. ->XXXV. Are these then all the keys,<-
->or do they offer even more<-
->others?<-

**Answer:** These are not yet all of them; there are also many more secret chambers to be unlocked. These are by no means the secret treasure-chambers of Nature, nor her end, nor shall anyone ever live who can explore or fathom all the treasures of Nature.

->XXXVI. What are the names of the even more secret<-
->keys, and who brings them?<-

**Answer:** The most secret key of all is the double **herald’s staff** <note>original: Herolds-Stab; refers to the Caduceus, the staff of Hermes/Mercury</note> of <term>Mercury</term> <note>original symbol: ☿</note>, with the two snakes, which the **Eagle**, the King’s bird, brings. The third key is the key to the **hellish dungeon**, the **fire against nature** <note>original: Feuer wider die Natur; a specific alchemical term for a corrosive agent or a heat that transforms matter differently than ordinary fire</note>, the poisonous dragon, the hellish bath; this is brought by the **Cold Bear** <note>In alchemy, the "Cold Bear" often refers to the North or to specific metallic minerals like antimony</note>. The fourth key is the red, bloody **Sun**, which the <term>Lion</term> <note>original symbol: ♌; referring to Leo, often symbolizing the "Red Lion" or sulfur</note> brings us.

...living waters in soul, spirit, and body; it multiplies these. The red, bloody sun makes it red, and it dries out the sea.

->XXXVIII. Can one then experience no greater wonders and secrets through this than making the <term>Tincture</term> <note>The concentrated essence or power of the Stone used for transmutation.</note> for the <term>Universal Stone</term>?<- <note>The legendary Philosophers' Stone, believed to transform base metals into gold and grant longevity.</note>

**Answer.** I have already said that this Stone is only the first treasure in the secret **treasure-chamber of Nature**, within the mineral kingdom. Whoever has once found this has found all the secret keys, by which he can strip Nature bare and look into the heart of everything under the whole heaven, upon and under the earth, and within the earth. Even more, he can look into the **astral air-world**, into the stars, into Paradise, into Heaven; indeed, he can thereby come before **God’s countenance**—that is, learn to rightly know and see Him, and in what kind of light He dwells—because such a person is illuminated with the <term>Light of Nature</term> <note>A Paracelsian concept referring to the innate wisdom and divine illumination found within the natural world.</note>, which light also comes from God.

->XXXIX. What then is the **double <term>Mercury</term>** <note>original symbol: ☿</note> called, when it hardens and coagulates with its salt?<-

**Answer.** It is called the **Crystal of <term>Mercury</term>** <note>original symbol: ☿</note>, the **Water- and Salt-Stone**, or the **Water-Stone of the Sages**, the glass-like <term>Azoth of Lull</term> <note>Named after Ramon Lull, a famous medieval philosopher; Azoth is the essential transformative agent in alchemy, often identified as the "mercury of the wise."</note>, and the **stony palace** into which the King can go and take up his residence. The **white <term>Sulfur</term>** <note>original symbol: 🜍; representing the active, masculine principle</note>, when it is sublimated, is sweet of taste.

<meta>This page continues the catechism-style explanation of alchemical terms and symbols. Following the previous page's discussion of the "Crystal of Mercury," it now defines the "Red Man," the "White Woman," and the "Green Lion," which represent various stages and substances in the creation of the Philosophers' Stone.</meta>

<page-num>41</page-num>
<header>* ):( o ):( *</header>

# XL. What then is this called, when it has been <term>fixed</term> <note>To "fix" a substance in alchemy means to turn a volatile or liquid substance into a solid form that can withstand fire without evaporating.</note>, or <term>calcined</term> <note>Reduced to a fine powder through the application of high heat.</note>?

**Answer.** It is called the **glassy sea**, which can be mingled with fire; the **natural treasure**; the **stone of the first order**; and the **first tincture on white**. It appears somewhat greenish; its taste is harsh, which however afterwards turns into a sweetness.

# XLI. What then is the red <term>tincture</term> <note>A potent essence or "medicine" capable of transforming base metals into gold or silver.</note> called?

**Answer.** It is called the **first tincture on red**, the **first stone on red**, and the **bloody stone**.

# XLII. What then is the **red man**?

**Answer.** It is the **red oil**, the <term>element of fire</term> <note>original: "Elementum ignis"</note>, from the **philosophical <term>vitriol</term>** <note>original symbol: ⊕; here referring to the purified mineral salt used as a starting material</note>, the **coral-red juice**.

# XLIII. What then is the **white woman**?

**Answer.** The **white <term>sulphur</term>** <note>original symbol: 🜍</note> of Nature, which is now dried out; the **white moonlight**.

# XLIV. What then is the **green <term>lion</term>** <note>original symbol: ♌; a common alchemical symbol for a raw mineral or a powerful acid capable of dissolving metals</note>?

**Answer.** It is the innermost **vitriol**, the **fixed salt** of the **vitriol** <note>original symbol: ⊕</note>, which is called the **little green lion**, in the innermost entrails of whose belly the **red stars** have their seat. It is also the **first white tincture**, which is called the **natural treasure**, and is made from this double salt. This is also called the **green lion**: the **first oil of <term>copper</term>** <note>original symbol: ♀</note> from <term>lead</term> <note>original symbol: ♄</note>, which is of the nature and character of copper, and is called **"the green moth turns"** <note>original: "tinea viridis girat"; likely a symbolic reference to a corrosive process that "eats" through metal like a moth through cloth</note>, through which the <term>mercury</term> <note>original symbol: ☿</note> is **precipitated**. These are green in their interior and also come to the **white tincture**, because through the **oil of lead** <note>original symbol: ♄</note> the **mercury** is made fixed and transformed into the **glassy sea**.

<sig>Ccc 5</sig>
<meta>catchword: XLV.</meta>

<meta>This page continues the alchemical catechism, transitioning from the basic mineral components to the symbolic marriage of the "Lions" and the definitions of the celestial symbols—the Sun and Moon—within the laboratory process.</meta>

<page-num>42</page-num>
<header>❧ ) : ( ☉ ) : ( ❧</header>

### XLV. ->What then is the green and red <term>Lion</term> <note>original symbol: ♌︎; the "Green Lion" often represents a raw mineral or a strong acid, while the "Red Lion" represents the same substance after further purification or heat.</note>,<-
->who enter into a bond of friendship with each other,<-
->as the starred <term>Lion</term> <note>original symbol: ♌︎</note> with the<-
->Crown?<-

**Answer.** It is the **white tincture**, which is united with the **red <term>Lion</term>**, so that from the mouths of both the **golden juice** flows out; the white tincture becomes the red, and the **green <term>Lion</term>**, as the rising **Sun** <note>original symbol: ☉</note> or the sun that is still hidden behind the moon, is transformed into the **red Sun**, into the **red tincture**. Then the white tincture is endowed with the name of the **starred green <term>Lion</term>**, to distinguish it from the previous salt or **green <term>Lion</term>**, which is also called **visible gold** <note>original: "aurum perspectibile"</note>.

### XLVI. ->What then is the Sun and the<-
->Moon?<-

**Answer.** This is understood in many senses. Firstly, it is our **moist Moon**, which we also call **Virgin's Milk**, which is hidden in our **red earth**; also our **white sulfur** <note>original symbol: 🜍</note> is called the **Moon**. Likewise the **white tincture**. Furthermore, that which is called our **Sun** is the **Sun-blood** before it is yet extracted from the red earth, and afterwards, when it has become the **red transparent Sun**, this is also our Sun, the **foliated earth** <note>original: "geblätterte Erde"; a stage where the material appears in thin, leaf-like layers or scales.</note>. Likewise, the **red <term>fixed</term> sulfur** <note>original: "figirte 🜍"; a substance that has been made stable and resistant to heat.</note> and the **red tincture**. The true **Sun**, however, is the composed and fixed **matter of the stone**, when the **Mercury** <note>original symbol: ☿</note> transforms into our rising **Sun**, and afterwards when such a **Sun** is colored yellow and red.

### XLVII. ->What then is the crowned Queen?<-

**Answer.** It is the **white tincture**.

<meta>This page continues the alchemical catechism from page 42, further defining the symbolic figures and substances encountered during the "Great Work." It focuses on the various forms of Mercury and the transition from liquid to solid states.</meta>

<page-num>43</page-num>
<header>❀ )( 0 )( ❀</header>

### XLVIII. ->What then is the crowned King?<-
**Answer.** The **red tincture** <note>the final, most potent state of the alchemical medicine, symbolized as a king because of its "rule" over the lesser metals</note>.

### XLIX. ->What then is the Virgin’s Milk?<-
**Answer.** It is the **Pontic water** <note>original: "pontiſche Waſſer"; likely referring to a bitter or sea-like mineral solvent</note>, the simple <term>Mercury</term> <note>original symbol: ☿</note>, and the **minor circulate** <note>original: "circulatum minus"; a refined alcoholic or mercurial solvent</note> when it is sublimated with its own salt.

### L. ->What then is the Eagle?<-
**Answer.** It is the <term>Philosophers’ Mercury</term> <note>original: "☿ Philoſophorum"; the volatile principle of the matter, often depicted as an eagle because it "flies" or evaporates easily</note>.

### LI. ->What then is the foliated earth?<-
**Answer.** It is the <term>Mercury</term> <note>original symbol: ☿</note> which has been released from its watery dampness and has stepped out of the sea onto dry land <note>this describes the point where the substance crystallizes or forms a solid, flaky residue</note>.

### LII. ->What then is the starred Eagle?<-
**Answer.** Precisely this **double Mercury**, which is dissolved in its oil and is called the **Great Circulate** <note>original: "groſſe Circulat"; a universal solvent reached through repeated distillations</note>, in spirit and body; the **water that does not wet the hands**, in which the **King and the Queen** <note>symbolizing the union of the active and passive principles</note> bathe.

### LIII. ->What then is Cyllenius with outstretched wings, whose two sides the Sun and Moon enclose?<-
**Answer.** Precisely this multiplying and increasing **solvent** <note>original: "Menſtruum"</note>, the **vegetable Water of Life** <note>original: "vegetabiliſche Lebens-Aqua-vit"</note>, which contains the powers of the **Sun** and the **Moon**. <note>"Cyllenius" is a classical epithet for the god Mercury, who was said to be born on Mount Cyllene; here it represents the winged, volatile spirit of the metal</note>

### LIV. ->What then is the Spirit of Mercury?<-
**Answer.** It is the raw, unripe, and untimely mineral spirit, which is nevertheless an eternal **solvent** <note>original: "Menſtruum"</note>; for it dissolves all its parts one after another, all of which must also be taken from it until they are all refined and purified through it.

<summary>This page defines high-level alchemical symbols including the "Crowned King" (Red Tincture) and various states of "Mercury" (Eagle, Cyllenius, and Virgin's Milk), explaining how the substance acts as a universal solvent.</summary>
<keywords>Red Tincture, Virgin's Milk, Philosophers' Mercury, Foliated Earth, Starred Eagle, Great Circulate, Cyllenius, Menstruum, Water of Life</keywords>

### LV. ->What then is the winged dragon?<-
**Answer.** It is the simple <term>Mercury</term> <note>original symbol: ☿; in alchemy, wings symbolize volatility—the ability of a substance to evaporate or rise when heated</note>.

### LVI. ->What then is the wingless dragon?<-
**Answer.** It is the **fixed part** of the <term>Mercury</term> <note>original symbol: ☿</note>, and the dragon which remains contained within the earth <note>the "fixed" part is the solid residue that does not evaporate, representing stability</note>.

### LVII. ->What then is the nymph of the sea, who pours out blood and milk from her breasts?<-
**Answer.** It is the **daughter of Neptune** <note>original: "Neptuni Tochter"</note>, the **Virgin’s Milk**, appearing when the Red and the White come forth. For both come from one body: first it is milk, and when it hardens, it becomes **white <term>Sulfur</term>** <note>original symbol: 🜍</note>, which is afterwards adorned with the **Gold-Soul** to become yellow and red <note>this describes the color changes (the 'Great Work') from the white stage, or *albedo*, to the final red stage, or *rubedo*</note>.

### LVIII. ->What then are the three wells of everlasting water?<-
**Answer.** They are the **three Principles**: <term>Salt</term> <note>original symbol: 🜔</note>, <term>Sulfur</term> <note>original symbol: 🜍</note>, and <term>Mercury</term> <note>original symbol: ☿</note>. All three spring from the **metallic serpent** that lies in the green grass <note>the "green grass" often refers to the raw, "unripe" state of the mineral matter</note>. They are made from the <term>Sun</term> <note>original symbol: ☉; representing gold or the solar principle</note>, from which the Sun, Moon, and all the stars receive their powers, and from which the living waters are made—the **water of life** <note>original: "Aqua-vitæ"</note>.

### LIX. ->What then is the philosophical Sun?<-
**Answer.** It is **Apollo** in royal garments, the **double Mercury**, which has cooked itself into the Sun and climbed the royal throne through its own **Eagle** <note>Apollo represents the perfected Gold; the "Eagle" here refers to the process of sublimation or distillation that purifies the substance</note>.

### LX. ->What then is the three-necked serpent?<-
**Answer.** It is the poisonous **earth-serpent**, our **Subject** <note>original: "Subjectum"; the raw material at the start of the alchemical process</note>, which holds the three Principles within itself,

### LXI. What then is the Pelican, who
->sprinkles its young with blood?<-
**Answer.** It is our **red fiery Menstruum** <note>original: "Menstruum"; in alchemy, this refers to a powerful solvent capable of dissolving metals, not the modern biological term</note>, its children are the young, the **7 philosophical metals**, which come from its body, and having grown out of the **white mercurial water**, are the white and red **Sulfur** <term>🜍</term>, which it sprinkles with blood, makes alive through the soul, and transforms into its own red nature <note>The pelican is a classic symbol of the "Rubedo" or reddening stage, where the substance "feeds" the process with its own essence to achieve perfection</note>.

### LXII. What then is the greening ore?
**Answer.** It is the **philosophical Gold** <term>☉</term>, which grows in the glass <note>this refers to the crystalline growths or color changes observed during the chemical process in a sealed flask</note>.

### LXIII. What then is the Duênech?
**Answer.** Our **Sulfur-Oil** <term>🜍</term>, which fixes the stone <note>makes it stable and resistant to heat</note> and makes it fluid.

### LXIV. What then is the dragon, who
->passes through the sprouting of the suns?<-
**Answer.** It is the **double Mercury** <term>☿</term>, thus cooked in **Gold** <term>☉</term>, which has grown through it in its body.

### LXVI. What then is the meaning of the saying of Mary the Jewess, sister of Moses, a Palestinian, namely: *The smoke loves the smoke and is loved by the same in return. But the white herb of the high mountain*
->*encloses or contains everything*<-
->*within itself?*<-

**Answer.** The smoke is the **Mercury** <term>☿</term>, the smoking poison, when it dissolves <note>original: "solvirt"</note> its own brother, born from the mother's womb—which is its fixed part—and also makes it volatile, so that it becomes smoke along with it. For these two love each other, rise to the heights and return again to the ground, resting in each other's arms; the white herb of the high mountain encloses both within itself. By this, the **double Mercury** <term>☿</term> or the sublimated **leafy earth** <note>original: "Terra foliata"; a state where the matter appears in white, leaf-like flakes</note> is indicated, which holds these two within itself, when the previous two smoking firebrands have grown into one body.

[Al]chemist he becomes, just as nature does, so shall Art give birth to all metals; what does he mean by that?

**Answer.** Here he describes that Mercury <term>☿</term> should be united with its own Sulphur <term>🜍</term> if it is to become fruitful and give birth to metals. Sulphur <term>🜍</term> is the father, and Mercury <term>☿</term> is the mother; through the union of these two, they also conceive children. If the Mercury <term>☿</term> is pure and beautiful, and the Sulphur <term>🜍</term> as well, then it will be a glorious fruit of solar <note>pertaining to the Sun or gold</note> nature. If the Mercury <term>☿</term> is of the nature of Saturn <term>♄</term> <note>Lead</note>, then Luna <term>☽</term> <note>Silver</note> will come from it; but if the Mercury <term>☿</term> is of the nature of Mars <term>♂</term> <note>Iron</note> and Venus <term>♀</term> <note>Copper</note>, Sol <term>☉</term> <note>Gold</note> will come from it. If, however, it is of a solar nature, a Tincture <note>a concentrated medicinal or alchemical substance capable of transmuting metals</note> will come from it.

Thus, everyone must know what kind of Mercury <term>☿</term> he precipitates—whether he has the exalted one at the 7th stage, or one of a lower degree. I have nothing to do here with common metallic Mercury <term>☿</term>, but rather with our seven philosophical metals. If someone is fortunate enough to find the exalted Mercury <term>☿</term> and precipitates it with its own Sulphur <term>🜍</term>—born from the mother’s womb—then he immediately has a tincture, which colors much or little depending on whether these two are pure or impure. Yet he can elevate, illuminate, and color this precipitate in his own ferments, unguents <note>ointments</note>, and salves as high as he wishes; for he can color the first white tincture, and etch the colors in through his own oils so that they remain fixed and permanent: lemon-yellow, orange-yellow <note>original: "Pomerantzen-gelbe"</note>, rose-color, of such color as pomegranate seeds, red like spinels <note>original: "Spinetten"; a red gemstone often associated with rubies</note>, and finally ruby-red, thick-red, like clotted dried blood, until it is sufficiently saturated and wishes to take in no more. <meta>This page continues the technical instructions for preparing the alchemical Tincture, moving from the philosophical Mercury described on page 49 to the process of "fixing" it using mineral salts and the teachings of Ramon Llull.</meta>
