## The 58th Figure.
### The Sign or Motto of our Maiden.

Our Maiden tells you wonders with a strange vision, so that you should note everything with an attentive mind. From the North Wind, the Bear may give you warm fires, and from there the South Wind will bring Lionish waters. These mixed together, two will fly, after you have entrusted them to a hot furnace, and will return from the high places back to the lowest.

## Interpretation.

Our Maiden is the Maidenly Mercury (☿); the other <term>Menstrua</term> <note>original: "Menstrua"—not referring to biology, but to alchemical solvents or "waters" used to dissolve solid matter</note> follow her, like the Bear from the cold North Wind—the sharp seals of old <term>Saturn (♄)</term> <note>Saturn/Lead; often represents the cold, dark, "dead" beginning of the work</note>, his oil or fire. The South Wind, the white <term>Sulphur (🜍)</term> oil, brings the red Lionish waters. These two fires, mixed with one another, are opposites <note>original: "Contraria"</note>; they fly from each other. One is called the glowing, flaming sword of Saturn (♄)—his scythe, with which he clips the wings of Mercury (☿); the other is the glowing red fire, the Mercury of the Sun, the fire of the Sun. And all are guided by the Maidenly Mercury, who precedes them all,

...points out the others with his fingers, and through him they must be brought to light.

### The 59th Figure.
### The new Motto or Sign.

I am a beautiful goddess, born deep at the bottom of the sea, which through its course watches over the whole earth and travels around it; our breasts pour out two small streams, namely of blood and of milk, which you may well know; when these two are mixed, leave them to the light <term>Vulcan</term> <note>The Roman god of fire, representing the alchemical heat of the furnace</note> to prepare, and then the Moon and <term>Apollo</term> <note>The sun god, representing the final "Golden" or Sun stage of the alchemical process</note> will come to fulfill your wish.

### Interpretation.

The goddess who sprouted at the bottom of the sea, which watches over the whole earth through its course, is our water when it is <term>coagulated</term> <note>The process of turning a liquid substance into a solid or fixed state</note> with the earth. The breasts of blood and milk that she gives are the red and white <term>Mercury (☿)</term> <note>original: "☿"—the philosophical mercury, not common quicksilver, representing the essential spirit of the matter</note>; when these two are mixed by the philosophical fire, then both <term>Tinctures</term> <note>The powerful, concentrated essences capable of transmuting base metals into silver (White) or gold (Red)</note> will fulfill our wish.

### The 60th Figure.
### Another Sign or Motto.

While she sheds those tears, you shall torture the <term>Eagle</term> <note>A common alchemical symbol for volatility or the process of sublimation, where solids are turned into vapor</note>; then the weak <term>Leo (♌)</term> <note>original: "♌"—the Lion, representing the "fixed" or solid principle, often associated with gold or antimony</note> will fall down through a terrifying death; his blood is the greatest treasure in the whole world. You shall reconcile him with the Eagle by means of tears, and then you will become rich.

<summary>This page presents two alchemical allegories: the goddess of the sea who produces the red and white mercury (blood and milk), and the "torture" of the Eagle to obtain the blood of the Lion, symbolizing the refinement of the philosopher's stone.</summary>

For they bathe at the same time, and soon they are consumed by love, and are of your nature, like Salamanders.

### Interpretation.

As soon as the seven eagles and the <term>Lion</term> <note>original: "♌︎"</note> are put together, the Lion wounds the eagles so that they shed tears through his blood; from this it begins to sweat until it produces drops which appear somewhat bluish. But because the eagles fly at him many times and constantly, they also wound the Lion and are stained by his blood. This struggle and battle lasts until they have all fallen dead there—the Lion and the Eagle. The blood of this green <term>Lion</term> <note>original: "♌︎"</note> is mixed with the eagle’s tears, with the multiplying <term>Menstruum</term> <note>original: "Menstruo"—an alchemical solvent or "first matter" believed to dissolve solids and nourish the growing "stone"</note>, which consists of Soul, Spirit, and Body, and is the ferment of the <term>Moon</term> <note>original: "☽" — silver</note>. Thus shall the white <term>Tincture</term> <note>A concentrated medicinal or transformative essence</note> multiply itself, for the Lion and the Eagle bathe together in this fountain and are increased tenfold; they are of the nature of fire, like the Salamander of the red tincture, because it can now live in the fire like the Salamander, for the Moon or the white tincture has become of a hot nature.

## The 61. Figure.
### The First Matter.

I am the Dragon, and spew deadly poison from my body; the red lion together with the green <term>Lion</term> <note>original: "♌︎"</note> loves me. I am called by many the pleasant **Egg of Nature**, and I fly away if you do not bind me well.

I am the kind of color that you desire; I am also man and woman; I delight the human body and also, at the same time, all metals.

## Interpretation.

The First Matter is poisonous and deadly, for when the two dragons—the **Volatile** and the **Fixed**—are put together and are meant to kill one another, they spew out poison; yet they are called the pleasant **Egg of Nature** <note>The "Philosophical Egg" is the sealed glass vessel where the transformation occurs, as well as a term for the primal substance containing the potential for life.</note>. When these two dragons have become one, from this egg comes the Red and the White; the white and red <term>Antimony</term> <note>original: "♁" – here referring to the base mineral matter or the "Earth of the Wise"</note>, the green <term>Lion</term> <note>original: "♌︎" – often representing raw mineral acids or unrefined matter</note> and the red <term>Lion</term> <note>original: "♌︎" – representing the perfected or solar matter</note> love it. The green Lion fixes it into the white tincture; the red Lion brings it to the red tincture. It is the color that one desires; it allows itself to be made black, white, yellow, and red.

Here I lie, as if I were buried by coarse sand, and our **Spirit** flees with the **Soul** into the heights. I am like the **Raven** until the 14th light runs back; then I renew the shining scepter of my race.

...finally the whole grave becomes like coarse sand; the body, grave, and sand are all one, for the **Royal Corpse** is contained in all three. Because the vessel is closed, however, they are forced to give a new life back to the body, to transform the **black raven** into the **white dove**, or rather the **white swan** <note>These birds represent the shifting colors of the matter: the Raven is the blackness of decay (Nigredo), while the Dove and Swan represent the purified white stage (Albedo).</note>. This state can also be called the **King in his shirt** <note>original: "König im Hemde" — an alchemical image for the "Albedo" stage, where the matter is purified and white, like a clean linen garment, but not yet fully perfected.</note>, because they are white like spirits, or like bodies risen from the death; so is this shining King. Then he can be renewed, and his scepter become even more shining—that is, yellow transformed into **Gold** <term>☉</term> <note>The symbol for the Sun, representing the perfection of Gold.</note>, so that the yellow color is carried onto the white, just as painters paint yellow onto white, and red onto yellow. The yellow color is called the King in his shirt putting on the golden garment, through which he propagates his lineage.

## The 63rd Figure.
### The Second Operation.

When many and varied colors appear, I shall visibly emerge with a **red body**. No one in the whole world will be found equal to me, and the **Sun** and **Moon** <note>In alchemy, the Sun and Moon represent Gold and Silver, or the masculine and feminine principles that must be balanced.</note> shall be subjected to my hands. Moreover, the **power of herbs** will be subjected to us, through which the diseases of men are driven away from the human body.

# Interpretation.

Here it is described how, when the **Purple Mantle** <note>The "Purple Mantle" represents the *Rubedo*, the final red stage of the alchemical process, signifying total perfection and power.</note> is placed over the King’s golden piece, many varied colors appear in place of the blackness—such as red, violet-brown, yellowish-red, lightning-blue, and other bright, high colors. The more and the higher this King is clothed in purple, the richer and thicker these colors show themselves. The **Sun** <term>Gold</term> and **Moon** <term>Silver</term> are subject to this **Tincture** <note>The Tincture is the "Philosopher's Stone" in its transformative, liquid-like state, capable of changing base metals into gold.</note>; they must hide themselves and allow this Tincture to rule over them, because they are as nothing compared to it. The weak must give way to the strong because here a greater King has taken the throne. He heals all herbs, for he himself is the greatest **Vegetable** <note>original: "Vegetabile" — in alchemy, this refers to the "living" or "growing" principle that permeates all nature, not just plants.</note>, which circulates through all diseases because he contains the **Quintessence** <term>The "fifth essence" or purest core of all matter</term> of all things within himself.

## The 64th Figure.
### The Third Operation.

> *Behold, twice five men* <note>The "twice five men" (ten) likely refers to the ten steps or proportions of the matter required to reach the final multiplication of the Stone's power.</note> *put me to death; yet they are also forced to perish and go down with me through death. The Sun has compassion for me, and the Moon with a black face, and the rainbow—a sign from heaven—is present. Therefore, I wash all my enemies clean of their filth and skillfully bring forth the triumphant sound.*

<summary>This page describes the transition to the Rubedo (red) stage, where the alchemical Stone achieves its final "Purple Mantle" of perfection, surpassing the Sun and Moon in power. It introduces the Third Operation, symbolizing a final death and purification that results in a triumphant, universal medicine.</summary>

## Interpretation.

When the King is dissolved in ten parts of his water—the **Male Menstruum** <term>Menstruum: A philosophical solvent used to break down matter. In alchemy, "male menstruation" refers to the active, solar solvent, as opposed to the passive, lunar one.</term>—it is because we create two "waters": one for the **Solar Body** <term>Gold</term> and the other for the **Lunar Body** <term>Silver</term>. When the King rots in his water, both the solvent and the Royal Body die and decay together. However, because the King is of a noble nature, his spirit unites with those other spirits, and they both return into the body to wash and purify it. Finally, he is resurrected from death. When the **Rainbow** appears <note>The "Rainbow" or "Peacock’s Tail" (*cauda pavonis*) is a stage in the alchemical process where many colors appear, signaling that the "blackness" of decay is ending and the work is succeeding.</note>, a new and improved life is found; the King is greater and augmented—that is, he is **Multiplied** <note>Multiplication is the process of increasing the quantity and power of the Philosopher’s Stone.</note>.

## The 65th Figure.
### The Fourth Operation.

So that one may recognize the great virtues I possess, I soon thereafter repay the harm done to me with my riches. For even if the **Ravens** <note>The "Raven" represents the *Nigredo* or black stage, where the matter is "consumed" by decay.</note> consume the hostile bodies, their spirit and soul nevertheless remain behind constantly. I unite these with myself and return the **volatile to its fixed state** <note>original: "volatilische Fix" — The goal of alchemy is to "fix the volatile," meaning to make a gaseous or spiritual substance solid and permanent.</note>, so that everyone may see the gifts of my right hand.

## Interpretation.

This is essentially the same operation as before, though it is carried out more fully here: the fixed spirit reclaims the body, elevating and increasing it, both in **quantity and in quality** <note>original: "in quantitate & in qualitate"</note>, so that it may [its]...

### The 56. Figure.
#### Fifth Operation.

I arise in the form of a Queen from the weight of the grave, and I bring joyful news to my brothers. The chaste Bridegroom has commanded me, the Bride, to lie with you, so that through my gifts I may renew your sorrowful hearts. This is the **White Tincture** <term>White Tincture: The penultimate stage of the alchemical work (Albedo), which has the power to transmute base metals into high-quality Silver.</term>, the White Queen, who precedes the Red, and transforms all metals into white **Gold** <note>The symbol $\odot$ represents the Sun or Gold. "White Gold" in this context refers to Silver that has reached a state of solar perfection.</note> when she is fermented with common **Gold**. For the White Tincture is the "Woman clothed with the Sun" <note>A reference to Revelation 12:1, a common alchemical trope for the perfected matter.</note>, and is herself the white **Gold**. She has also been dissolved in **Royal Water** <term>Royal Water: Usually *Aqua Regia*, a powerful acid, but here it refers to a philosophical solvent composed of Soul, Spirit, and Body.</term> and therein increased in both quantity and quality.

...it becomes one again, which was also one before; but now with a thousandfold improvement. Old **Saturn** <term>Saturn (♄): Representing lead and the beginning of the Work; often depicted as a restrictive father or a jailer who holds the raw materials of the Stone in "prison."</term> is the beginning, who sired all of this and held it hidden within his body; this Saturn holds the key. They were all kept in his prison and lay captive, but the Work is made from **Mercury (☿), Sulphur (🜍), and Salt (🜔)** <note>Known as the *Tria Prima*, these are the three primary substances of the universe according to Paracelsus.</note>. The Mercury rises of its own accord through wondrous steps and stairs to the heights until it takes the royal seat; for in its beginning it is a naked, miserable child, full of exposure and moisture. But when its water is dried up by its own Salt, then it is the herb that serves against death. It takes the place of both man and woman because of its double body <note>A reference to the "Rebis" or Divine Hermaphrodite, a symbol of the union of opposites.</note>, and is killed by its own poison and wrath—that is symbolic—and afterwards is colored white, yellow, and red in its own oils, so that it becomes the fluid, incombustible oil.

...the dragon's gifts, for they come from him; the stiffened **Bear** <note>original: *starrende Bähr*; referring to the constellation Ursa Major, the Great Bear, which symbolizes the cold, fixed, and "stiff" nature of the North and the element of Earth/Saturn.</note> is the fire of **Saturn** (♄), which the beautiful **Apollo** <note>Apollo represents the Sun or solar heat.</note> brings to the **Eagle**, the **Mercury of the Philosophers** (☿). ## The 70th Figure.
### The Coat of Arms of Our Hero.

Our hero, after he has overcome all his enemies in war, now possesses the greatest reward for his virtue as a conqueror. For he represents the **Sun**, surrounded by beautiful rays, and beneath this are the **Horns of the Moon**; in the center, three round **Apples** adorn the Sun, and these signs will reveal to you the origin along with the deeds.

### Interpretation.

The hero is our **King**, the **Red Tincture** <term>Red Tincture: The final, perfected stage of the Alchemical Work, also known as the Philosopher's Stone, which has reached the highest degree of power and "glow."</term>, the Son of the Sun. He has laid low all his enemies on water and land. He is the Sun and the Moon, within both of which he stands surrounded by radiance, for the Sun and Moon were his parents. Beneath him are the **Horns of the Moon**, the waning moon, which can no longer be seen because of the Sun's brightness. The three round **Apples** are the **Three Principles** <note>Mercury, Sulphur, and Salt; the fundamental components of all matter in the Paracelsian tradition.</note>. These give the work and its origin their powers and their entire capacity.

LXXIX.

## The 71st Figure.
### The Matter of the Philosophers' Stone.

It is indeed only one thing, but there are also two of them, yes, even three; whoever is wise will surely learn that I have not told him this merely to pass the time. For it is only one Dragon, of little value and old in years, which possesses the King's Well and the correct reward. There are two <term>Mercuries</term> <note>symbol: ☿</note>, of which you must unite the constant or fixed with the flying. It is one soul, one body, and one light spirit.

## Interpretation.

Our Matter is but one single matter, wherein is our beginning and end. There are also two of them, namely Husband and Wife, the active and the passive <note>original: *agens und patiens*; the masculine and feminine forces that drive the chemical reaction.</note>; there are also three, the three Principles: <term>Salt</term> <note>symbol: 🜔</note>, <term>Sulfur</term> <note>symbol: 🜍</note>, and <term>Mercury</term> <note>symbol: ☿</note>. And yet it is only a single Dragon, the old, poisonous, and smoking <term>Saturn</term> <note>symbol: ♄; in alchemy, Saturn often represents the "lead" or the base, raw state of matter that must be purified.</note>, who holds the King's Well—the rich treasure—in his keeping. There are two <term>Mercuries</term> <note>symbol: ☿</note>, the Fixed and the Flying. Brother and sister, both of whom must become one body; it is one soul, the red blood; one body, the double Mercury; one light spirit, called the oil or air-spirit <note>original: *Lufft Spiritus*</note> of Mercury.

Thus far have the figures, symbols, texts, and sayings of M. Stolcius <note>Referring to Michael Stolcius von Stolcenberg, a 17th-century physician and poet known for his alchemical emblem book, the *Viridarium Chymicum*.</note> been interpreted. Now follows the continuation of the questions that were begun.

<summary>This page defines the "Matter" of the Philosophers' Stone through the paradox of being one, two, and three things simultaneously, concluding the section on Stolcius's alchemical emblems.</summary>
<keywords>Materia, Philosophers' Stone, Dragon, King's Well, Mercury, Salt, Sulfur, Saturn, Michael Stolcius, Alchemical Principles</keywords>

--- Page 120 ---
<meta>This page continues the series of alchemical questions and answers following the emblems of Michael Stolcius. The previous page concluded the formal interpretations of the "71st Figure" regarding the Matter of the Stone and the nature of the Dragon and Mercury. This page addresses the common complaint that alchemical writing is intentionally "dark" or obscure.</meta>

->* ):( 0 ):( *<-
<meta>Signature: Hhh 3. Catchword: men (likely the first syllable of 'menschen' or humans on the following page).</meta>

LXXIX. All these texts are difficult and obscure, as is their interpretation; can they not be presented more simply?

**Answer:** They are neither difficult nor obscure; rather, everything is clear and easy. It is merely hidden from those to whom God does not wish to grant it—those who should not possess it, for they would use it wrongly. For God looks upon the heart; He searches the heart and the kidneys <note>original: *Hertz und Nieren*; a biblical idiom referring to a person’s innermost thoughts and secret intentions.</note>. 

However, it is not possible for this to be described more clearly. For things that are not naturally found upon the earth, but are instead shown through the <term>Sons of Art</term> <note>original: *Söhne der Kunst*; a traditional title for alchemists who "perfect" nature through their work.</note>, have no names and no parents. They are new births and forms of nature, arising from the first, second, and third <term>purification</term> <note>The stages of refining raw matter into a more spiritual or potent state.</note>, and from so many <term>compositions</term> <note>original Latin: *compositionibus*</note>. 

From one thing come the <term>elements</term> <note>original Latin: *elementa*</note>; these reveal the three <term>principles</term> <note>original Latin: *principia*; typically Salt, Sulfur, and Mercury.</note>; the three principles reveal the metals; these are transformed into bright-shining stars and into so many kinds of <term>tinctures</term> <note>Powerful alchemical liquids capable of transmuting metals or healing the body.</note>. For this, one must have many kinds of <term>solvents</term> <note>original Latin: *menstrua*; in alchemy, this refers to a liquid that dissolves a solid body to extract its essence.</note>—sometimes wet, sometimes dry spirits—as well as salt, water, oil, and fire. 

What are all these called, and how should one name them? And yet all of these come from a single thing. It reminds me of a man who is to beget many children with his wife; the children are now in his loins, but who sees them? Or who would count them, how many they shall be, or what they shall look like? They likely all look like humans, but no two faces are alike; one resembles the father, the other the mother; one is of a more masculine, the other of a more feminine nature; they come

...humans, who yet come from one father and one mother. So it is also here in our work: where Saturn [♄], Jupiter [♃], and Mercury [☿] have the upper hand, they beget a feminine body, which is called Luna <note>The alchemical name for Silver.</note>; but when Mars [♂] and Venus [♀], acting as the Earth <note>The symbol ♁ refers here to the "Earth" or "Antimony," representing the material base.</note>, have the upper hand, they beget a masculine body, which is called Sol <note>The alchemical name for Gold.</note>. And Mercury [☿] is helpful to both parts, for he is the **Mediator** <note>original Latin: *Mediator*; the go-between that reconciles opposites like hot and cold or male and female.</note> who unites both and contains them within himself; he is the man, and also the woman, as one chooses.

Now, when the ancient masters wished to instruct those who follow through their writings, they had to devise something that could be compared to such things, giving these "children" names according to their nature. When the matter is still unripe, sour, deadly, and poisonous, it must be called Serpent, Dragon, Basilisk, Poison, Dragon’s Blood, Toads, or Adders. When it possesses sharpness and is medicinal, it is also called Milk, Wine, and Blood. But when it has become heavenly, it is the food of the gods: **Nectar** and **Ambrosia**.

If it is <term>tinctured</term> <note>original: *tingiret*; the state where the substance has the power to "tinge" or transform other metals into gold or silver.</note> or of a metallic nature, it carries the names of the planets as they grow according to their degrees. In short, everyone has given it names according to its kind, in whatever <term>livery</term> <note>original: *Liberey*; the outward appearance or "uniform" a substance takes during different stages of the process.</note> he found it, yet always corresponding to its nature. 

The wet, simple Mercury [☿] they called **Virgin’s Milk** <note>A common term for a white, acidic liquid used as a solvent.</note> or the **Milk of Birds**. The double Mercury they have called * <note>The asterisk in the original likely indicates a missing term or a reference to a marginal note.</note> the double salt, the **Hermaphrodite**, the moon, the white sun, sun and moon in one body, the white Earth [♁], the foliated earth, the starry eagle, the flying dragon, **sublimated mercury** <note>original Latin: *☿ sublimatum*</note>, the **mercury of mercury**. If it was <term>fixed</term> <note>rendered stable and resistant to heat, no longer evaporating.</note>, they called it the tamed eagle, the slain...

Thus they pondered whether they could not increase it even further through the repeated work of <term>imbibition</term>? <note>original: *Eintranckung*; the process of "feeding" the dry alchemical stone with liquid to increase its potency.</note> But they found that once it was saturated, it would not take up more of its own "flesh and blood" or <term>solidify</term> <note>original: *coagulirte*; the transition from a liquid to a solid state.</note> with it. [ve]getable kingdom; And herein is the true description of the entire **universal menstruum** <note>original Latin: *Menstrui universalis*; a universal solvent believed to be able to dissolve any substance, especially for the creation of the Philosopher’s Stone.</note>; they could not have described it better. For who can separate water from water, and oil from oil?

Therefore, it endures in all kinds of fire-resistant metals and makes them resistant as well; the more they suffer the fire, the more firmly it creeps into them, thus protecting them from the fire, and the metals with it. Because of its oiliness, it is also called the **first essence of gold**, because gold is so "oily," <note>In alchemical theory, "oiliness" or "fatness" refers to the presence of internal sulfur, which provides flexibility and substance, whereas other metals were seen as "dry" or brittle.</note> while all the others are hard and brittle.

to separate therefrom, because they are not of a metallic nature; nor is the **Spirit of Vitriol** <note>original symbol: 🜔; a strong acid produced by distilling green vitriol (ferrous sulfate).</note>, for although much of the **Universal Spirit** <note>original: *Spiritu universali*; the life-force or "breath" of nature believed to be trapped in matter.</note> is contained within it, this **acid** <note>original: *Acidum*</note> is nevertheless **remote matter** <note>original: *Materia remota*; a starting material that is too chemically distant from the desired goal to be practical.</note>, and our lives would be too short if we wished to make a **tingeing stone** <note>The Philosopher's Stone, so called because it "tinges" or transforms base metals into gold.</note> from it. 

In nature, we have a much closer matter, which we purify in this acid without destroying the **fundamental moisture** <note>original: *Grund-Feuchtigkeit*; also known as "radical moisture," the essential fluid that sustains the life and flexibility of a substance.</note>. However, no internal purification happens through this; rather, only the external coarse body is separated from the **ore-matrix** <note>original: *Berg*; literally "mountain," referring to the rocky mineral or gangue in which the metal is found.</note>, so that the external and coarse slags fall away. This is done so that it can afterwards be purified through its own **water and vinegar**, for the entire **body** <note>original: *Corpus*</note> does not serve this work. 

First, one takes the ore from the matrix by means of the vinegar; this is not counted as a true step, nor is it called a **solution** <note>original: *Solution*</note>. Instead, our first true solution occurs when its own **vinegar** is distilled off from this matter, and its own body—the **red Laton** <note>original: *rothe Laton*; a term for the impure metallic "body" (often associated with lead or copper) that must be cleansed of its "redness" or darkness to reach the white stage of the work.</note>—is dissolved and purified within it. This is its first purification, by which it is made suitable to be separated into the **elements** <note>original: *Elementa*</note>. Then, the **confused chaos** <note>original: *Confusum Chaos*; the initial, unorganized state of the matter at the start of the alchemical process.</note> must rot in its own water. Afterward comes the purification of the elements, followed by the **separation of the pure from the impure** <note>original: *Scheidung Purum ab Impuro*</note>, which transcends the elements, and finally the proper **composition**.

**Answer.** The **Matter** <note>original: *Materia*</note> is the flower of the earth, indeed of all metals; it possesses much **arsenical poison** <note>Arsenic was frequently associated with the "first matter" of the Stone due to its ability to "whiten" copper, though it was known to be deadly.</note>, yet it is not as poisonous therein as it is in **cobalt** <note>original: *Cobold*</note>, **water-horse** <note>original: *Wasser-Roß*; likely a local miner's term for a specific arsenical or cobalt-heavy mineral, possibly arsenopyrite.</note>, or **red silver ore** <note>original: *roth gülden Ertz*; pyrargyrite.</note>, because these still contain a terrifying **acid** <note>original: *Acidum*</note>, which is why the former acts so vehemently. If an animal body receives it into the stomach, it creates black **burn-spots** <note>original: *Brandt-Flecke*; refers to necrotic tissue or gangrene caused by the corrosive poison.</note> wherever it settles, and it also kills. If one throws it upon **Copper** <note>original symbol: ♀ (Venus)</note>, it turns it white, but it immediately tarnishes in the air and becomes black. In contrast, our **arsenic** <note>original: *Arsenick*</note> derived from **Lead** <note>original: *Bley*</note> is somewhat milder and sweeter; it can be taken in a small dose for **chest diseases** arising from viscous humors, where it immediately causes a **fermentation** and lifts such things through an **emetic** <note>original: *Vomitiv*; a substance used to induce vomiting to clear the "viscous flows."</note>, but it mostly carries them out from below. In cases where otherwise nothing but death and instantaneous suffocation were to be hoped for, it is given in only half to one full grain, and well-tempered through **Spirit of Wine** <note>original: *Spiritum Vini*; concentrated alcohol or ethanol.</note>. Also, such a substance does not turn **Copper** <note>original symbol: ♀</note> black; however, once it has been turned into **Oil**, it is a **Basilisk** <note>A mythical serpent whose breath or gaze was fatal; here used to describe a highly concentrated, lethal chemical state.</note>, and far more poisonous than before—it is a **tinging poison** <note>original: *venenum tingens*; a poison capable of "tinging" or deeply penetrating and transforming substances.</note>. Its **Mercury** <note>original symbol: ☿; the "hidden spirit" or metallic essence extracted from the matter.</note>, however, is without poison and is the wonder of nature; its **red oil** is the **blood of metals**, **Dragon’s Blood**, and the **Blood of the Lion** <note>original symbol: ♌; the Lion represents Gold or the "fixed" principle in alchemy. Dragon’s Blood refers to a deep red color achieved in the process.</note>. But all these things are not yet the **High Medicine**, although they already possess more power than all the shopkeeper’s pills in small and great illnesses which are already fixed and deep-rooted. Yet they are even more magnificent when they become sweet, heavenly, fragrant, glowing, and burning—the **flowers of the Sun and the Moon** <note>The "flowers" of Gold (Sun) and Silver (Moon), representing the purified metallic essences.</note>, the raw, unripe u...

... premature <term>Gold <note>original symbol: ☉; representing the Sun or the metal Gold.</note></term>, indeed all seven metals, and all plants <note>original: *Vegetabilia*</note> that are plucked in their beautiful bloom, possess their greatest power. For the bloom is their summer; their ripe fruit or seed is their autumn; and thereafter they wither, which is their winter. Now, flowers certainly have more scent and power than their seeds; here, in this flower or **Royal Herb** as well—which might rightly be called the **white lily** and **red rose**, as well as the **yellow sunflower** <note>These flowers represent the stages of the alchemical "Great Work": the white (albedo), the red (rubedo), and the golden completion.</note>—this substance is of a contrary nature compared to other herbs. It grows in winter; in the cold it is constricted <note>original: *constringirt*</note>; in the black earth it rots and develops a black root, which disguises itself in all colors. Then the **white lily** begins to bloom, the **Royal Regalian Herb** that serves against death. Then comes the **sunflower** and the **red rose**, which is dipped in blood. Through much labor, its power increases infinitely—not that it possessed this full capacity before (although even without processing <note>original: *Wirckung*; literally "effect" or "operation"</note> it is not without many virtues), but it receives much more through the work. For when the **red mineral spirit** is added to its body, these two adverse fighters burn into one another—namely, the dry, hot, mineral salt and the cold <term>Lead <note>original symbol: ♄; representing the planet and metal Saturn/Lead.</note></term>—and from them both, a perfect metal is formed. Both of these already possess a different nature than they did before, once the substance is again dissolved in its first **solvent** <note>original: *Menstruum*; a technical term for a liquid capable of dissolving a solid, often referred to as the "alchemical womb."</note>. It is then another body altogether, one degree higher in power and virtue, and this process is continued until it reaches the highest degree of nature.

Through its heavenly light and fire, it illuminates everything, expels all diseases, ignites the spirit of life, strengthens, increases, doubles, and indeed restores manifold what was lost; it preserves the <term>Radical Moisture <note>original: *Humidum radicale*; the vital fluid or "oil" that was believed to sustain life and determine longevity.</note></term> into old age and protects against all diseases. Through Art, it is brought to such a high state; from Nature alone, it does not possess such strength.

<meta>This page continues the technical and philosophical explanation of the alchemical process. LXXXIII. Why then does Hermes say: The Sun is its father, the white Moon its mother, the Air—the Wind—has carried it in its belly, the Earth has nursed it? <note>This is a direct quote from the *Tabula Smaragdina* or Emerald Tablet, the foundational text of Western alchemy.</note>

**Answer.** The Sun is its father, which is our fire, the Solar Sulphur <term>🜍 <note>The active, masculine principle of heat and soul.</note></term>. The mother is the white Moon, our solvent <term>Menstruum <note>original symbol: ☽</note></term>, which we also call Virgin’s Milk <note>A common alchemical term for a white, nourishing liquid or precipitate used during the "White" stage of the work.</note>. The wind has carried it in its belly because it was carried in the round vessel on the wings of the wind as a subtle dusting <note>original: *Gestiebe*</note>. The earth has nursed it; she has raised it, and it has grown and become great within her. These are our elements <term>Elementa</term>.

LXXXIV. Is there then not more than one way to separate this raw body from the coarse mountain, or are there more?

**Answer.** There are multiple ways, and much superfluous work <term>Superflua</term> is done with this matter; yet it must be purified so that its radical moisture <term>humidum radicale <note>The essential, life-sustaining fluid within a substance that prevents it from becoming brittle or "dead."</note></term> does not burn away. This moisture is its growing little root; for if one burns its Mercury <term>☿</term>, it cannot afterward dissolve <note>original: *solviren*</note> its own body <term>Corpus</term>. And if its Sulphur <term>🜍</term> is burned to dead earth, then this dead earth cannot refine <note>original: *gradiren*</note> the Mercury <term>☿</term>, nor soften it, nor make it pourable, fluid, and into an incombustible oil.

LXXXV. But how can its Mercury <term>☿</term> produce such a wondrous solution <term>Solution</term>, and how does that happen?

**Answer.** We see this in common Mercury <term>☿ <note>Quicksilver.</note></term>, how it, as a dry body, dissolves <note>original: *solvirt*</note> the metals,

<summary>This page provides a symbolic interpretation of the Emerald Tablet of Hermes, defining the Sun as Sulphur and the Moon as a solvent (Virgin's Milk). It emphasizes the importance of preserving "radical moisture" during purification to ensure the substance remains capable of transformation into a medicinal oil.</summary>

<keywords>Hermes Trismegistus, Emerald Tablet, Sulphur, Mercury, radical moisture, Virgin's Milk, solvent, elements, purification</keywords>

<meta>This page continues the technical instructions on the preparation of the alchemical Mercury. Here, the author describes the superior dissolving power of "our Mercury" compared to common mercury, and its transformation into the "Universal Solvent" and the "Water of Life."</meta>

<page-num>128</page-num>
<header>❁ ):( 0 ):( ❁</header>

But if it is sublimated through salts <note>original: *Salia*</note>, it is even more [effective]; but when the corrosive substance is sublimated, dissolved by the Spirit of Salt <note>original: *Spiritum Salis*; an early name for hydrochloric acid</note>, and finally rises over as an oil, it dissolves even better. So also our Mercury <term>☿</term>, when it is the Virgin’s Milk <note>A term for a white, purified liquid state of the matter</note>, it dissolves the Silver <term>☽ <note>original: Luna</note></term>, stays with it, and becomes silver. Common mercury <term>☿</term> does not do this; even if it precipitates itself with the silver, when it is placed upon the cupel <note>A shallow, porous vessel used in refining to separate precious metals from lead or mercury through high heat</note>, it departs again, and one has no more silver than before. Why? It only clings to the silver <term>☽</term> from the outside; it carries no Lunar Sulfur <note>The active, internal "soul" of silver</note> within it as ours does. Because ours softens the silver, crawls—so to speak—into all its veins, ennobles itself thereby, and because it is of one essence with it (or its primary water), it stays with it and thus becomes a permanent body of Silver <term>☽</term>.

But our double Mercury <term>☿</term> is far more exalted and of far greater power. When it has been sublimated into the foliated earth <note>original: *Terra foliata*; a flaky, purified state of the matter often compared to "white earth"</note>, it is the Full Moon, the white Earth <term>♁</term> that dissolves all metals radically <note>original: *radicaliter*; meaning "to the root" or "fundamentally"</note>. It is the true universal solvent <term>Menstruum universale</term>; it is the [substance] that crawls into the metals, seeks the heart, and makes them both volatile and fixed, according to how one directs the work. It is white on the outside and blood-red on the inside, adorned with all colors; it takes on the metallic bodies and becomes one with them. 

But when this [substance] is dissolved in its own oil and has itself become an oil, then it is the permanent water <term>Aqua permanens</term>, the blessed water <term>Aqua benedicta</term>, the Water of Life, and the incombustible oil. In this state, the Mercury <term>☿</term> has been brought into an oil; it is revivified <note>original: *revificirt*</note> in this water, restored in Mercury <term>☿</term>—in spirit, soul, and body—and the Spirit of Mercury <term>Spiritum ☿</term>. What wonders are to be performed with this water, no man will believe...

LXXXVI. **But how can the philosophical Sun <term>☉</term> and Moon <term>☽</term> have different parents than common Gold <term>☉</term>, seeing as it is nonetheless equal to common Antimony <term>♁ <note>original: Antimonii</note></term>, which displays the Sun of the Great World?**

Answer: The philosophical Sun <term>☉</term> is better than common Gold <term>☉</term> and brings it into a Tincture <note>A potent alchemical liquid used for transmutation or healing</note>. It dissolves <note>original: *solvirt*</note> common Gold <term>☉</term> because it still retains its vital spirit <note>original: *Lebens-Geist*</note> within it; common Gold <term>☉</term> is dead, while the philosophical is alive. This noble Sun of ours has its origin from the Sun and the Spirit of the Great World <note>the Macrocosm</note>, just as common Gold <term>☉</term> does; however, Nature has placed all her powers into our Subject <note>original: *Subjectum*; the raw starting material of the Alchemical Work</note>. The Sun has its own essence; this "little sun" was entrusted to the keeping of Saturn <term>♄ <note>original: Saturnus; representing lead or the dark, heavy beginning stage of the work</note></term>, who has locked it away as if imprisoned in a dungeon, and it is only to be released with great toil and labor. Beside this Sun, the woman Moon <term>☽ <note>Silver</note></term> is also to be found. Nature and Art have shown how the Moon should be transformed into blood—that is, to become the Sun—and then to ferment that Sun with the Sun, with our red liquid Sun. Then it is the Son of the Sun, and this certainly has nobler parents than common Gold <term>☉</term>, which, though it grew from the same arsenical spirits and from the elements of the great

LXXXVII. **Are the elements, then, which are the beginning of the Stone, different from the elements of the Great World <note>the Macrocosm, or the universe at large</note>?**

Answer: They are indeed far more magnificent than the elements of the Great World. For the Spirit of Nature has placed the Quintessence <note>the "fifth essence," the purest and most concentrated form of a substance</note> of all powers into this mineral body, which contains all of Nature and its effects within itself, as well as the spirits of the planets. It is an extract of the Great and Small Worlds <note>the Macrocosm and the Microcosm</note>, the flowers of the Sun <note>Gold</note> and the Moon <note>Silver</note>. We purify this body of its coarse mountain <note>the "mountain" refers to the raw, unrefined mineral ore or dross</note> through a bath that is not contrary to its nature; afterward, we dissolve <note>original: *solviren*</note> it in its own waters, separating the confused chaos <note>original: *confusum Chaos*; the initial state of the matter where elements are mixed and disordered</note> into the elements. From the previous body we have taken only its blood and soul—the heart, its innermost part—and separated these into the elements. These are now certainly of a completely different form and heavenly compared to the elements of the Great World, for those [macrocosmic elements] are the mothers of this Mineral <note>original: *Minera*</note>, the Mi-

LXXXVIII. But does it require very great effort, danger, and a long time to complete this work?

**Answer:** Yes, it requires a fair amount of time and great understanding to undertake the compositions <term>Compositiones <note>original: "Compositiones." The mixing and preparation of chemical substances.</note></term>, especially in the first stage. This is when one wishes to create the double Mercury <term>☿ <note>Mercury. Here, it refers to a "double" or philosophic mercury, a refined solvent.</note></term>, such that the salt is dissolved and its spirit is drawn out of it. The body <note>The solid matter or "residue" in a reaction.</note> is capable of nothing until after its solution <term>Solution <note>original: "Solution." The process of dissolving a solid into a liquid.</note></term> and volatilization <term>Flüchtigmachung <note>The process of turning a solid into a gas or vapor so it can be distilled.</note></term>. However, the salt itself must be unlocked by its own key, which is the water through which it is purified. At first, however, it coagulates with this body until it softens and dissolves it in its innermost core; then it is very bitter and inflamed, and it gives off colored vapors. This bitter taste is of a solar <note>Gold-like</note> and Venusian <term>♀ <note>Venus, representing Copper.</note></term> nature. There is also something sweet, which comes from Mars <term>♂ <note>Mars, representing Iron.</note></term>, so that this bitterness is not so horribly perceived; otherwise, it would be even more bitter. The leaden <note>Saturnine/Lead</note> and Jovial <note>Jupiter-like/Tin</note> bodies were separated from it in the first extraction, and only the colors of the other planets were taken. These have ascended <note>Distilled or risen as vapor.</note> and carried over the blood and soul to the solar, martial, and Venusian bodies. This was done by the "thief" Mercury <term>☿ <note>Mercury, often called a "thief" in alchemy because it "steals" or absorbs the properties and "souls" of other metals.</note></term>, who has robbed them all so that their bodies remained naked and bare. ### XC
**Question:** Are there then no shortcuts in this work?

**Answer:** Yes, there are shortcuts to make the **red Mercury** <note>☿</note> ripe and gold-like. By using **golden Sulfur** <note>represented by the symbol 🜍</note>, this water is turned into a mire and then into earth. Yet this **wet Mercury** must first dissolve its own salt and separate from its "earthiness" <note>original: "Terrestrität"</note>. One must thereafter dissolve such **fixed precipitate** <note>A solid that has settled out of a liquid solution.</note> in its own oil, and make it fixed again; then soften it with the air, pour the **red oil** over it, and set it to be fixed. The long duration required for this work is only to raise the **Mercury** to the **degree of Gold** <note>represented by the symbol ☉</note>.

### XCI
**Question:** I believe at last that the **Stone** is still possible to make, but is its **multiplication** <note>The process of increasing the quantity or power of the Alchemical Stone.</note> hard to find, if it is to be increased forever and eternally?

**Answer:** It is also not difficult. The **white Stone** is dissolved in the white oil or **Menstruum** <note>A powerful solvent; the term suggests a life-giving or transformative fluid.</note>, which consists of soul, spirit, and body; and the **red Stone** is dissolved in the **red oil**. These two are called the **Water of the Sun** and the **Water of the Moon**. However, there are still other types of **ferments** <note>Substances used to "leaven" or trigger the transformation of the matter.</note>, so that one allows it to rot <note>original: "faulen." This refers to putrefaction, a necessary stage of breakdown before rebirth.</note> anew...

...allows <note>original: "läßt." This completes the sentence from the previous page regarding making the matter fixed.</note>, and only then is it illuminated and multiplied; this path is more tedious, but it is also of greater power.

### XCII
**Question:** Now, if someone cannot find the great and secret **Work of Nature** <note>The "Magnum Opus" or the process of creating the Philosopher's Stone.</note> and cannot make the **Stone**, but has learned the **separation of the elements** <note>The process of breaking a substance down into its constituent parts: fire, air, water, and earth.</note> and does not know how to proceed with the **composition** <note>The reuniting of the purified elements into a new, perfect body.</note>, yet still wishes to produce a **tinging stone** <note>A substance capable of "tinting" or transmuting base metals into gold or silver.</note> so that they may have some benefit from it—how should they begin?

**Answer:** There are countless types of these paths. Who can recount them all? Whatever is cooked in these **waters** is improved. He who has learned to separate the elements possesses the **Water of the Sun** <note>Gold</note> and the **Water of the Moon** <note>Silver</note>, in both of which common **Gold** <note>represented by the symbol $\odot$</note> and **Silver** can be dissolved and, through repeated labor, be turned into **Tinctures**. Furthermore, in the **white water**, **Moon** <note>represented by the term "Luna" or silver</note> can be dissolved, and in the **red water**, **Sun** <note>Gold</note>. When these two are united together, the **Moon** <note>represented by the symbol $\leftmoon$</note> will be entirely transformed into the **Sun**.

### XCIII
**Question:** In what manner then does it dissolve the metals?

**Answer:** They are dissolved through it without violence, without the destruction of their **radical moisture** <note>original: "Grund-Feuchtigkeit." In alchemy, this is the essential, life-sustaining fluid thought to be found within all matter; destroying it would render the metal "dead" and untransmutable.</note>, by means of this fatty, restorative, and multiplying **solvent** <note>original: "Solvens"</note>. The metallic bodies are melted therein without any noise, just like ice in warm water; they remain as a slimy, fatty essence because this **menstruum** <note>A specialized alchemical solvent, named for its cyclical or "monthly" nature of preparation.</note> clings to them very firmly and cannot be separated from them.

<summary>This page explains an alternative alchemical path for those who can separate elements but cannot complete the full Philosopher's Stone, focusing on creating transmuting tinctures. It describes a gentle dissolution of gold and silver into a fatty, inseparable essence using specific "waters."</summary>

<keywords>separation of elements, Water of the Sun, Water of the Moon, Tincture, radical moisture, solvent, menstruum, transmutation, Gold, Silver</keywords>

If one wishes to draw off some of it according to the necessity found, the remaining body stays behind as a slime, upon which our **Oil**, as the **Air**—which is also called the **Spirit of Mercury** <term>Spiritus Mercurii: The volatile, life-giving essence or "spirit" extracted from a metal.</term>—can be poured. In this way, it will draw the **Sulfur** <term>🜍: In alchemy, Sulfur represents the soul, the active combustible principle, and the source of color.</term> from the metal into itself and transform it into the form of an oil. One joins the **Sulfurs** of the **Sun** <note>Gold</note> and the **Moon** <note>Silver</note> together, as well as their **Mercuries**; one digests them both, and then unites them. 

However, if someone—once these two bodies are united—can join them with another golden **Sulfur**, whether it be from **Lead** <note>represented by ♄</note>, **Copper** <note>represented by ♀</note>, **Iron** <note>represented by ♂</note>, or wherever else he wishes, to dye it red, and afterwards, when they are dry, dissolves them with their own **Sulfur** in the **Spirit of Mercury**, unites them with the red **ferment** and makes them **fixed** <note>Permanent and resistant to fire.</note>; such a person has a far-reaching **Tincture**. This way is short, and no man has yet taught this, which I now write for the benefit of my fellow man.

### XCIV. But does one need fire in these works, and also strong fire?

**Answer:** In the **solutions** <note>The process of dissolving solids into liquids.</note>, no; the heat should only be as gentle as the sun's warmth because of the volatility of these spirits—especially the **Oil** or **Air**, which must be very firmly sealed. Furthermore, the smell of this oil is dangerous and a true poison, which stains. In the **coagulation** <note>The thickening or solidifying of the liquid matter.</note>, however, when it is being composed, one must keep it in a **grading fire** <term>Gradier-Feuer: A refining fire used to increase the purity or "grade" of a substance through steady heat.</term> until it is fixed. Also, in this work, there is not such danger as in the **Universal Work**, where one must observe certain times, deadlines, days, and hours for when one wishes to **imbibe** <note>original: "einträncken." The process of adding liquid to the dry matter to nourish it.</note> it. But where one does not know such things, and

Here, however, in the **particular works** <term>Particular Works: Alchemical processes aimed at specific transmutations or medicinal results, rather than the "Universal" goal of the Philosopher's Stone.</term>, the soakings and **conjunctions** <note>original: "Conjunctiones"; the joining of chemical principles.</note> may occur whenever one thinks it is fixed enough; there is no danger. ### XCV. Would like to know the true Saturn explained even better?

**Answer:** **Saturn** <note>represented by ♄; alchemically associated with Lead.</note> is a son of Heaven and Earth, of the goddess **Vesta**, and a husband of his sister **Opis** <note>Ops, a fertility goddess.</note>, the helping and preserving power of all things; the ancient **Demogorgon** <term>Demogorgon: A primordial being in mythology often associated with the deep origins of the world and the chaotic "First Matter" of alchemy.</term>, who swallows his children and gives them back again. He is depicted with white-gray hair, in his hand a sickle or scythe, and a snake whose head bites its tail <note>the Ouroboros, representing cyclicality and eternity</note>, indicating his self-helping and regenerating nature. With the scythe, he cuts off **Mercury's** <note>represented by ☿</note> wings, and has a yellow belt around his body. In his heart and within, he is the **Sun** <note>represented by ☉; Gold</note>; he has red boots on his feet. His **fixed Salt** <note>represented by ♵</note> is our **red Venus** <note>represented by ♀; Copper</note>, the red earth, clay or loam, our Pole, and red **Atramentum** <term>Atramentum: A term for vitriol or dark metallic sulfates, often used in the production of tinctures.</term>.

### XCVI. Is the Quintessence of this lead then the true and universal solvent, and how is it made?

**Answer:** It is certainly the beginning of our work, and this **Old Man** <note>An alchemical personification of Saturn, or Lead, which was considered the "oldest" and heaviest of the metals.</note> must be led into his bath of purification so that the coarse, impure mountain <note>Referring to the "earthy" dross or mineral impurities in the raw metal.</note> falls to the bottom, and only the pure part is taken for our work. When it has been digested in vinegar and well-colored, it is poured off, and more vinegar is added until it will no longer take any color. The vinegar is then evaporated until it reaches the thickness of honey, and then distilled step-by-step until the **retort** <note>A glass vessel with a long, downward-pointing neck used for distilling substances.</note> glows and gives off no more vapors. The spirit that has passed over is then **rectified** <note>Purified through re-distillation.</note>; contained within it is a spirit which burns like **spirit of wine** <note>Alcohol; indicating a highly volatile, flammable liquid.</note>, a white **phlegm** <note>A watery, tasteless distillate produced during the early stages of heating.</note>, and finally a red oil. This oil must be driven over with a strong fire and protected from the air, otherwise it all evaporates. All these things are raw, poisonous, and not yet medicinal, and one must be wary of them. This volatile, raw spirit of <term>Saturn ♄</term> I and others call the **Spirit of Mercury** <term>Mercurius ☿</term>, which is our first secret key.

### XCVII. Now I would also like to hear about the red oil of Antimony? <note>original: ♁; the symbol for Antimony.</note>

**Answer:** **Paracelsus** <note>The famous 16th-century Swiss-German physician and alchemist known for his "spagyric" or chemical approach to medicine.</note> describes this quite clearly when he says: "Take our **Antimony of Venus** <note>original: "♁. veneris"; likely referring to an alloy or preparation involving both Antimony and Copper.</note>, prepared to the highest **Spagyric** <term>Spagyric: An alchemical method of medicine-making that involves separating a substance into its components (sulfur, mercury, and salt) and then recombining them in a purified state.</term> degree, having previously retained the element of **Water** and **Air** <note>original: "Aquæ und aëris"</note>, and dissolve it," etc. So far Paracelsus. Our red **Antimony of Venus**,

...essence <note>Completing the phrase "red essence" from the previous page.</note> thus played with <term>Saturn ♄</term> <note>The metal Lead, associated with the planet Saturn.</note>, dissolve it in our **Spirit of Mercury** <term>☿</term>, which is our distilled vinegar or our *Aqua fort* <note>original: *Aqua fortis* or "Strong Water"; a potent mineral acid.</note>, which is also the poisonous snake and dragon. It is an exceedingly beautiful **solution**, but in this solution it soon begins to rot and stink; it has the smell of dead bodies, and the beautiful color will be lost and become like shoemaker’s blacking <note>Known as the *nigredo* stage, this blackening was seen as a necessary "death" or putrefaction of the matter before it could be purified.</note>. Shake and rattle this many times so that the red fixed **Fire** <term>△</term> <note>In this context, the "sulfur" or the non-volatile internal essence of the substance.</note> dissolves in its own oil or **Spirit of Mercury** <term>☿</term>, and the blessed oil is brought to its **Quintessence**. 

It must stand for a long time and be shaken often. When it begins to become wonderfully beautiful in color and smell, it is time to distill. This **philosophical Sun** <term>☉</term> <note>The perfected state of the matter, often called "Philosophical Gold."</note>—the dry red **Mercury** <term>☿</term>—when it begins to resolve into its own essence, will show all the colors of a rainbow at the edge of the glass, most beautiful. Then, when one distills it, the **tartaric** <term>🜔</term> <note>Derived from Tartar, a salt found in wine barrels; here it refers to a specific crystalline distillate.</note> part rises over like tears, round as drops that hang in the glass. The **Spirit of Mercury** <term>☿</term>, however, comes over as an oil and colors the **helm** <note>The "alembic" or the top cap of the distilling apparatus.</note> yellow. Finally, the blessed red oil comes over and colors the **helm** red with wonderful colors. Every- <note>Catchword: "Every-" (German: Jeden).</note>

Either these parts are collected individually, or each is **rectified** <note>Purified through re-distillation.</note> on its own. For he who has no **Metallic Salt** cannot make the **Mercury of the Philosophers**. This **Salt** becomes volatile and turns into the **foliated earth** <note>original: *geblätterten Erden*; a crystalline state that looks like tiny leaves or scales.</note>. One pours its own **Oil** onto this volatile earth, whereby it becomes bound and **fixed** <note>Chemically stable and resistant to heat.</note>. Through the **Air** it becomes yellow and fluid, but through the **Fire** it becomes red. These things could have been presented somewhat more briefly; however, because I did not want to bring the curse of all the **Adepts** <note>original: *Adeptorum*; the masters of alchemy who swore to keep their secrets from the unworthy.</note> upon myself for making these matters too common, I have observed several rules so that only the worthy—those who devote themselves to the study of books and to **practice** <note>original: *Praxis*; hands-on laboratory work.</note>—may find it...

### XCVIII. What does one do now with this Earth or metallic salt?

**Answer:** One brings it to the **Cup of Love**, the **Universal Spirit**, and so this **Virgin Earth** <term>Jungfräuliche Erde</term> <note>The purified solid matter or "salt" remaining after distillation, seen as a receptive female principle.</note> is impregnated. It brings forth fruits of its own kind because she is a pure virgin, the **Queen Alma** <note>original: *Alma*; Latin for "nourishing" or "kind." In this context, it refers to the "Soul of the World" or the purified matter ready for transformation.</note>. When she is impregnated with the **Spirit** <note>original: *Spiritu*</note> or the **Universal Menstruum** <term>Menstruo universali</term> <note>The "universal solvent" of the alchemists, capable of dissolving bodies back into their primary essence.</note>, there grow from her womb small **metallic sparks**, and finally the purest and most per- <note>The text cuts off mid-word, likely "perfect."</note>

<summary>The author emphasizes that alchemical success requires a charitable heart and divine favor, excluding the greedy. He then describes the "impregnation" of the purified "Virgin Earth" by the "Universal Spirit" to produce metallic offspring.</summary>

...[most perfect] metals. The Earth, when it is moistened with the **Spirit** <note>original: *Spiritu*</note>, is very dry and parched; therefore, it soaks up this water like a **sponge** <note>The original German *Scham* is likely a transcription error or archaic variant of *Schwamm* (sponge), reflecting the earth's thirst for the liquid spirit.</note>. During the first soaking, because there is so little water, it will not smoke—or at least so little that one does not perceive it. When the water has **congealed** <note>original: *geronnen*</note> within it, one moistens it again; then it begins to **simmer** <note>original: *brodnen*</note> and give off vapors, but not yet wetness; rather, it produces smoke, steam, and mist. When it is soaked even more, the Earth is finally softened, and the **mercury** <term>☿</term> **amalgamates** <term>amalgamiret</term> itself with the salt or the fixed part of the Earth. It becomes like a **thick butter** <note>In alchemy, a "butter" refers to a substance with a dense, oily, or pasty consistency, such as "butter of antimony."</note>; one continues this as the opportunity arises until the entire Earth has captured the **mercury** <term>☿</term> within itself and appears as a **shining mercury** <term>☿</term>. With further soakings, the Earth is **dissolved** <term>solvirt</term>. First, the water necessarily had to be **coagulated** <term>coagulirt</term> because there was much water but little Earth, so this water had to become Earth. But now, the work must be reversed. That which **coagulated** must be **dissolved** <term>solvirt</term> again and become water. As soon as this Earth is **dissolved**, it turns green, which is the **blessed and growing Green** <note>Often called the "Green Lion," this color symbolizes the arrival of life, vitality, and the "vegetable" power of the stone.</note>. The vessel will color itself like a **green walnut leaf** with a golden luster, the **gold-mirror** <term>Gold-Spiegel</term>, and many beautiful colors, especially yellow and red. It smells dry and bitter, even quite disgusting. These colors prove that this Earth is becoming **volatile** <note>Turning from a solid into a gas or a refined liquid.</note>, and the Spirit, which lay bound within it,

...a foul odor, which is no longer bitter, but reeks of rot like a corpse. This black **Laton** <term>Laton: An impure metal or alloy, often representing the "body" of the matter that must be purified.</term> is the **Copper of Hermes** <note>original: *Æs Hermetis*</note>, the **foliated earth** <term>geblätterte Erde: The purified solid matter that takes on a flaky or layered structure.</term>; this must be cleansed of its blackness and filth, and the head is taken away from this **black raven** <note>The "Raven's Head" (*Caput Corvi*) is the traditional alchemical name for the darkest point of the *nigredo* stage.</note> so that the **white dove** <note>A symbol for the *albedo* stage, representing purification and the appearance of the white stone.</note> comes in its place. There was very little salt when it was purified in its **phlegm** <term>Phlegmate: The watery, tasteless liquid that remains after distillation.</term>; once it became white, it was increased. Now, however, when the blackness is taken away from the animal that dwells in this thicket, it increases by means of the **white spirit** with which this blackness is washed away. 

The blackness does not arrive all at once, but first becomes sooty on top, as if coal dust or black pepper were scattered upon it, until it becomes blacker and blacker; the thicker it gets, the blacker it becomes. But before it turns black, it becomes yellow like **amber** <note>original: *Agtstein*</note>, and also red. One need not worry about the blackness, for it will surely come; but when it is washed away by the **white milk**, the whiteness appears. Then the raven must not be let back in, but the white dove may certainly return and bring the **olive branch** <note>original: *Oehl-Blat* (oil leaf/olive leaf). A biblical reference to the end of the Great Flood, signifying that the volatile "waters" have subsided and the matter is stabilizing.</note> in its mouth. 

One must not stop the whitening, because one cannot do too much here, for **Silver** <note>original symbol: ☽</note> cannot become too white. One must also pay close attention to when this bird wishes to fly; if one waits until it pleases the bird itself, it will fly away and leave behind an empty nest. I have, however, already considered in this book what signs occur when this rock shall tear open, and this stone...

It details the physical changes in the matter, including the foul smell of decay and the specific way colors shift from yellow and red into a deep, sooty blackness before being washed white.</summary>

...stone is ground down, one does not wait so long; take some of it and throw it onto a glowing metal plate. If it rises up <note>i.e., sublimes or vaporizes</note> and leaves little or nothing on the plate, then sublime it into the **foliated earth** <term>Terram foliatam: The "leafy" or "layered" earth, a stage where the matter becomes purified and flaky.</term>. Divide this into two parts: one for **multiplication** <note>The process of increasing the power or quantity of the alchemical medicine.</note>, and the other for the **Stone**.

### XCIX. How is the multiplying ferment made?

Answer: The **mercury** <note>original symbol: ☿</note> sublimated over seven nights is dissolved in its own **Aqua Fortis** <term>Aqua Fortis: Nitric acid, literally "strong water."</term> oil or air, and the spirit is drawn off from this. This is the true **Philosophical Aqua Regia** <term>Aqua Regia: Literally "royal water," a powerful solvent capable of dissolving gold.</term> and the **mineral bath**. When the **mercury** has been turned into an oil—into a water that does not wet the hand <note>A traditional description of the "mercurial water," a substance that is liquid but does not behave like common water.</note>—it is the true **grading oil** <term>Gradier Oehl: An oil used to increase the "grade" or purity and color of a metal.</term>. This is the bath in which the **Man and Woman** <note>Allegorical figures representing the active (Sulfur/Gold) and passive (Mercury/Silver) principles.</note> can bathe themselves, for it dissolves and joins them together.

### C. What is done thereafter?

Answer: One makes the other composition, namely that of the **Stone**.

### CI. How is the same made?

Answer: The **mercury** <note>original: ☿</note> must be made **fixed** <note>In alchemy, to "fix" a substance is to make a volatile or vaporous substance solid and resistant to heat.</note>; then it is our **white earth** <note>original symbol: ♁, usually representing Antimony or the Earth.</note>.

### CII. Is this then the Stone, when they say: "Sow your Gold into the foliated earth"?

Answer: Yes, it is, when one sows the **gold** <note>original symbol: ☉</note> into its own earth. Before this, we passed unnoticed through a very secret door of the **first conjunction** <term>Conjunction: The chemical union of two substances, often personified as a marriage.</term>. This wedding was held in the **House of Nature**, where **Heaven and Earth** <note>Representing the volatile/spiritual and fixed/physical aspects of the work.</note> closed together and climbed upon the **throne of friendship**; this wedding was held for a quite a long time...

<summary>This page details the preparation of the "ferment" and the "mineral bath" used to unite the chemical principles. It describes the symbolic "marriage" of Heaven and Earth, signaling the successful union of the volatile and fixed aspects of the Great Work.</summary>
<keywords>multiplication, ferment, mercury, Aqua Fortis, Aqua Regia, mineral bath, conjunction, foliated earth, fixation</keywords>

...after <note>Continuing from the previous page's "thereaft-"</note> he had been [cleansed], meanwhile **Mercury** <note>original symbol: ☿</note> had to circle this heaven seven times, along with the **Sun** <note>original symbol: ☉</note>, **Moon** <note>original symbol: ☽</note>, and other **Planets**. **Mercury**, however, has the most to do, for he was the messenger for them all and held the key to this **Wedding-House** <term>Hochzeit-Hause: An alchemical metaphor for the laboratory vessel where the union of different substances—the "chemical wedding"—takes place.</term>, which he had received from **Saturn** <note>original symbol: ♄</note>. **Mercury** was now the friend of them all, for he was helpful to all of them: when they had made themselves dusty and dirty through too much movement, or had become overheated, he bathed them, washed them, gave them drink, and opened one secret chamber after another so that the gods and goddesses could come together and dally with one another. Because they all commit adultery in this **Wedding-House**, even brothers and sisters sleep with one another <note>This "incestuous" union refers to the joining of chemical principles that originate from the same primary matter.</note>. 

However, **Mercury** himself becomes exhausted through so much work, effort, and running, so that he falls to the earth and becomes earth as a punishment for sins. He is sown into the earth and becomes himself the **black earth**. This earth is black, but quite lovely; the **Sun** has burned it so much until he is washed clean of **original sin** <note>In alchemy, "original sin" often refers to the impurities or "grossness" inherent in raw matter.</note>, bathed white and pure, dried off again, and has begun his course into heaven. Because he has attained new life through this death, he has become the **Crowned Eagle** <term>Crowned Eagle: A symbol for a highly purified, volatile substance that is capable of "soaring" or sublimating without losing its power.</term> who can fly into the **Sun**. And yet, this secret key was not taken into account because I made no fuss about it. **Mercury** was sown into the earth; but now, when he has become the **flying earth** <note>A substance that has been sublimated into a light, airy state.</note>, he becomes the field in which the **Sun** or the **serpent’s teeth** are sown.

However, he must first unlock the **Center of the Earth**, which is as hard as steel and diamond and cannot be forced by any power—not even the greatest—and all arrows shot at it will only bounce back. The goddess **Vesta** <note>Vesta, goddess of the hearth, symbolizes the hidden, internal fire residing within the "rock" of the raw matter.</note> has her castle in this rock; but **Mercury**, as a cunning and swift spirit who has flown through the heavens, brings **heavenly water**. With this, he gradually softens this earth and hard rock until he has deeply buried the **Spring-Root** <note>The "Spring-Root" (Springwurzel) is a mythical plant from folklore said to blast open any lock or mountain; here it represents the chemical force that ruptures the "body" of the metal from the inside.</note> that bursts this castle open, for it is its nature that it must be opened from within. **CX.** What, then, does Mercury <term>☿: Mercurius. The fluid, volatile spirit that drives the alchemical process.</term> unlock with the key he received from Vesta <note>In this context, Vesta represents the fixed, terrestrial "body" of the matter.</note>?

### CXI. What treasure then is kept within the King's locked chamber?

**Answer:** There are pure jewels and crowns inside, made of pure precious stones and each fashioned from a single piece—not patched together here and there—and of such high value that no artist <note>In an alchemical context, "artist" refers to the practitioner or alchemist attempting to replicate nature's work.</note> can afford them. The golden crowns here are esteemed only as lead; one is a magical crystal mirror through which one can see the innermost parts of the earth and all its treasures. Whoever can prepare such crowns is a <term>Magus: A master of the hidden laws of nature; an alchemical initiate.</term>, who can see into the heart of all things, into the whole of Nature, and into Paradise. Whoever possesses these can laugh and be joyful until his death. The second crown is made of a whole pearl; because of its preciousness, it cannot be valued, nor can it be bought, even if one were <note>Croesus was the King of Lydia in the 6th century BCE, legendary for his immense wealth.</note> Croesus himself. The Golden Fleece <note>A reference to the Greek myth of Jason and the Argonauts, often used by alchemists as a symbol for the recovery of the "gold" of the Great Work.</note> is also kept here. The third crown is of opal, containing all colors within it through the many <term>uniones: From the Latin *unio*, referring to large, singular pearls of extraordinary quality.</term>; this crown is worn by the White Queen <note>The "White Queen" refers to the *Albedo*, or the white stage of the alchemical process, representing purification.</note>. After that, one finds crowns of jasper, of ruby, one of an entire <term>carbuncle: A deep red gemstone, often identified with garnet, historically believed to emit its own light in the dark.</term>, and yet another of a heavenly radiance, before which even the carbuncle appears dark. [It does] not show its wonders if it is not resolved and dissolved, and spreads itself throughout the entire heaven; this is the true Treasure-Chest of the King. The key is heavenly; a son of the gods possesses it. For this most secret key conjugates the fire with the new heaven and the new earth, because the former has passed away and all has become new <note>This is a reference to the New Jerusalem in the Book of Revelation (21:1), which alchemists used to describe the "New Earth" or the perfected Stone after the "old" corruptible matter has been purified by fire.</note>. And because the sun now divides itself again and has occupied the whole heaven, it burns no longer, nor does it set, nor is it eclipsed; for this heaven shines of itself, and the <term>Mediator: An intermediary substance or spirit, often identified with Mercury or the "Spiritus Mundi," that reconciles opposing elements like fire and water.</term> has stepped back into the center. He has brought the Arctic Pole <note>original: *Polum arcticum*</note> to the Antarctic Pole <note>original: *Polo antarctico*</note>, and thus brought them into one, and united this with the astral aerial-heaven, conjugated it with the fire, and established peace between the adversaries <note>The "adversaries" are the conflicting qualities of nature, such as heat and cold or dryness and moisture, which are reconciled in the Alchemical Wedding.</note>. Thus, the old adversity is set aside, everything has progressed to rest and stillness, the earth has become heaven, and the heaven brought to earth, and everything is illuminated with the heavenly light. This is the art of boiling fire in water <note>The "boiling of fire in water" is a classic alchemical paradox describing the union of the fixed (fire) and the volatile (water), or the creation of a "liquid fire" (the Elixir).</note>.

<summary>This page describes the final stage of the alchemical Great Work, where a heavenly key is used to unite fire and water, resulting in a transfigured "new heaven and new earth" where all opposites are reconciled.</summary>
<keywords>Treasure-Chest, Solar-Fire, Mediator, Arctic Pole, Antarctic Pole, astral heaven, boiling fire in water, reconciliation of opposites</keywords>

### CXII. Earlier, the Stygian Water <note>original: *Aquæ Stygiæ*; named after the River Styx, this refers to a powerful, corrosive alchemical solvent or "corrosive water" used to break down substances.</note> was mentioned; what is it, how is it used on the Dragon, and who is the Dragon?

**Answer:** The ever-watchful Dragon is the <term>Universal Mercury: The fundamental "spirit" or "prime matter" that alchemists believed could be extracted from any substance to begin the transformation into gold.</term> <note>original symbol: ☿</note>, which blows fire and smoke from its nostrils. At first, there are two: one without wings and the other with wings; these guard the golden apples in the Hesperian Garden without ever sleeping. Jason must contend with these on his journey and voyage to the island of Colchis <note>original: *Colchos*</note> if he wishes to retrieve the Golden Fleece. When he has brought his <term>Labors: The specific chemical operations or "stages" of the alchemical process.</term> to an end, he must kill this single Dragon—which has swallowed the other into its own body—and then suffocate it in the "hellish water," which is called the "fire against nature" <note>original: *Feuer wider die Natur*; a liquid that acts like fire by dissolving solids.</note>. When this prepared "soup" is poured on and the Dragon is dissolved <note>original: *solvirt*</note> and fixed <note>original: *figirt*; made solid or non-volatile</note> within it, then Jason has won; he can take the Golden Fleece and restore his old father's health. 

**Note:** The fire-breathing Dragon is not the Sun <note>original symbol: ☉; representing Gold</note>. Medea further demonstrates the fermentation with the paste <note>original: *Pasta*</note> of this Dragon; after this final work, Jason saw what a double treasure he possessed: both wealth and health. Cadmus also kills the Dragon, and he must be Jason’s companion, for without him Jason would have accomplished nothing; Cadmus is himself the serpent.

### CXIII. What then is the Philosophers' Vinegar <note>original: *Acetum philosophorum*</note>?

It is Virgin's Milk <note>original: *Lac Virginis*; a common alchemical term for a white, milky liquid or precipitate produced during the cooling or dilution of certain chemical solutions.</note>.
